<div className="bible-chapter" lang="en" dir="ltr" data-lang="en" data-translation="nmv" data-translation-id="eng_nmv" data-translation-label="NMV" data-book="EXO" data-book-slug="exodus" data-book-name="Exodus" data-chapter="1" data-verses="1213">

<div className="bt">
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 2:1–2; Genesis 46:8–26; Genesis 35:23–26"> Now these &#91;are&#93; the names of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, which came into Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;; every man and his household came with Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 35:22"> Re’uven &#91;Behold a Son&#93; &#91;Behold a Son&#93;, Shim’on &#91;he who hears&#93;, Levi (Levite) &#91;Adhesion, Joiner&#93;, and Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 28:20; Genesis 35:23"> Yissakhar &#91;There Is Recompense&#93;, Z’vulun &#91;Glorious Dwelling Place&#93;, and Binyamin &#91;Son of the Right Hand/Strength&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> Dan &#91;Judge&#93;, and Naftali &#91;My Wrestling&#93;, Gad &#91;Good Fortune with Troublesome Effort&#93;, and Asher &#91;Happy&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Genesis 46:26–27; Judges 8:30"> And all the souls that came out of the waist of Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93; were seventy souls: for Yosef &#91;(God) He Shall Add&#93; was in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; &#91;already&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 50:26; Genesis 50:24; Acts 7:14–16"> And Yosef &#91;(God) He Shall Add&#93; died, and all his brethren, and all that generation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Genesis 46:3; Genesis 1:28; Deuteronomy 26:5"> And the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; were fruitful, and increased abundantly, and multiplied, and waxed exceeding mighty; and the land was filled with them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Acts 7:18; Ecclesiastes 2:18–19; Ecclesiastes 9:15"> Now there arose up a new king over Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, which knew not Yosef &#91;(God) He Shall Add&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24–25; Ecclesiastes 4:4; Jacob 3:14–16"> And he said unto his people, Behold, the people of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; &#91;are&#93; more and mightier than we:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Tehillim - Psalms 83:3–4; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:11"> Come on, let us deal wisely with them; lest they multiply, and it come to pass, that, when there falls out any war, they join also unto our enemies, and fight against us, and &#91;so&#93; get them up out of the land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 15:13; Genesis 47:11; Exodus 3:7"> Therefore they did set over them taskmasters to afflict them with their burdens. And they built for Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; treasure cities, Pitom &#91;City of Justice&#93; and Ra’amses &#91;Child of the Sun&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> But the more they afflicted them, the more they multiplied and grew. And they were grieved because of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:20"> And the Mitzri made the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; to serve with rigour:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Numbers 20:15; Exodus 2:23"> And they made their lives bitter with hard bondage, in mortar, and in brick, and in all manner of service in the field: all their service, wherein they made them serve, &#91;was&#93; with rigour.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And the king of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; spoke to the ‘Evri’ot &#91;Hebrew&#93; midwives, of which the name of the one &#91;was&#93; Shif’rah &#91;Beautiful&#93;, and the name of the other Puah &#91;Splendid&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Exodus 1:22; Gosel 21:38; Revelation 12:4"> And he said, When you2f do the office of a midwife to the ‘Evri’ot &#91;Hebrew&#93; women, and see &#91;them&#93; upon the stools; if it &#91;be&#93; a son, then you2f shall kill him: but if it &#91;be&#93; a daughter, then she shall live.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 5:29; Exodus 1:21; Gosel 10:28"> But the midwives feared God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and did not as the king of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; commanded them, but saved the men children alive.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 8:4; 2 Samuel 13:28"> And the king of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; called for the midwives, and said unto them, Why have you2f done this thing, and have saved the men children alive?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 2:4–24; 2 Samuel 17:19–20; 1 Samuel 21:2"> And the midwives said unto Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93;, Because the ‘Evri’ot &#91;Hebrew&#93; women &#91;are&#93; not as the Mitzri &#91;Egyptian&#93; women; for they &#91;are&#93; lively, and are delivered before the midwives come in unto them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Isaiah 3:10; Ecclesiastes 8:12; Mishlei - Proverbs 11:18"> Therefore God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; dealt well with the midwives: and the people multiplied, and waxed very mighty.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="1 Kings 11:38; 1 Samuel 2:35; Tehillim - Psalms 37:3"> And it came to pass, because the midwives feared God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, that he made them houses.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:16; Exodus 1:16"> And Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; charged all his people, saying, Khav’vah &#91;Eve&#93; (to breath, to live) every son that is born you2f shall cast into the river, and every daughter you2f shall save alive.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 2:1–2; Genesis 46:8–26; Genesis 35:23–26"> And there went a man of the house of Levi (Levite) &#91;Adhesion, Joiner&#93;, and took &#91;to wife&#93; a daughter of Levi (Levite) &#91;Adhesion, Joiner&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 35:22"> And the woman conceived, and bore a son: and when she saw him that he &#91;was a&#93; goodly &#91;child&#93;, she hid him three months.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 28:20; Genesis 35:23"> And when she could no longer hide him, she took for him an ark of bulrushes, and daubed it with slime and with pitch, and put the child in it; and she laid &#91;it&#93; in the flags by the river’s brink.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> And his sister stood afar off, that is to say what would be done to him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Genesis 46:26–27; Judges 8:30"> And the daughter of Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; came down to wash &#91;herself&#93; at the river; and her maidens walked along by the river’s side; and when she saw the ark among the flags, she sent her maid to fetch it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 50:26; Genesis 50:24; Acts 7:14–16"> And when she had opened &#91;it&#93;, she saw the child: and, behold, the babe wept. And she had compassion on him, and said, This &#91;is one&#93; of the ‘Evrim &#91;Hebrew&#93; children.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Genesis 46:3; Genesis 1:28; Deuteronomy 26:5"> Then said his sister to Par’oh’s &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; daughter, Shall I go and call to (to do entirely, do wholly) youi a nurse of the ‘Evri’ot &#91;Hebrew&#93; women, that she may nurse the child for youi?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Acts 7:18; Ecclesiastes 2:18–19; Ecclesiastes 9:15"> And Par’oh’s &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; daughter said to her, Go. And the maid went and called the child’s mother.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24–25; Ecclesiastes 4:4; Jacob 3:14–16"> And Par’oh’s &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; daughter said unto her, Take this child away, and nurse it for me, and I will give &#91;youi&#93; youri wages. And the woman took the child, and nursed it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Tehillim - Psalms 83:3–4; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:11"> And the child grew, and she brought him unto Par’oh’s &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; daughter, and he became her son. And she called his name Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;: and she said, Because I drew him out of the water.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 15:13; Genesis 47:11; Exodus 3:7"> And it came to pass in those days, when Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; was grown, that he went out unto his brethren, and looked on their burdens: and he spied an Mitzri &#91;Egyptian&#93; attacking an ‘Evri’ot &#91;Hebrew&#93;, one of his brethren.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> And he looked this way and that way, and when he saw that &#91;there was&#93; no man, he killed the Mitzri &#91;Egyptian&#93;, and hid him in the sand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:20"> And when he went out the second day, behold, two men of the ‘Evri’ot &#91;Hebrew&#93; strove together: and he said to him that did the wrong, For what reason strike youi youri fellow?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Numbers 20:15; Exodus 2:23"> And he said, Who made youi a prince and a judge over us? intend youi to kill me, as youi killed the Mitzri &#91;Egyptian&#93;? And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; feared, and said, Surely this thing is known.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> Now when Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; heard this thing, he sought to slay Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;. But Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; fled from the face of Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93;, and dwelt in the land of Midyan &#91;Strife&#93;: and he sat down by a well.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Exodus 1:22; Gosel 21:38; Revelation 12:4"> Now the priest of Midyan &#91;Strife&#93; had seven daughters: and they came and drew &#91;water&#93;, and filled the troughs to water their father’s flock.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 5:29; Exodus 1:21; Gosel 10:28"> And the shepherds came and drove them away: but Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; stood up and helped them, and watered their flock.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 8:4; 2 Samuel 13:28"> And when they came to Reu’el &#91;Friend of God&#93; their father, he said, How &#91;is it that&#93; you2f are come so soon to day?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 2:4–24; 2 Samuel 17:19–20; 1 Samuel 21:2"> And they said, An Mitzri &#91;Egyptian&#93; delivered us out of the hand of the shepherds, and also drew &#91;water&#93; enough for us, and watered the flock.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Isaiah 3:10; Ecclesiastes 8:12; Mishlei - Proverbs 11:18"> And he said unto his daughters, And where &#91;is&#93; he? why &#91;is&#93; it &#91;that&#93; you2f have left the man? call him, that he may eat bread.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="1 Kings 11:38; 1 Samuel 2:35; Tehillim - Psalms 37:3"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; was content to dwell with the man: and he gave Moshe his daughter.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:16; Exodus 1:16"> And she bore &#91;him&#93; a son, and he called his name Gershom &#91;Exiled One&#93;: for he said, I have been a stranger in a strange land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And it came to pass in process of time, that the king of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; died: and the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; sighed by reason of the bondage, and they cried, and their cry came up unto God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; by reason of the bondage.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; heard their groaning, and God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; remembered his covenant with Avraham &#91;Exalted Father&#93;, with Yitz’chak &#91;Laughter&#93;, and with Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; looked upon the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and God-Elōhīm &#91;The Creator&#93; had respect unto &#91;them&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 2:1–2; Genesis 46:8–26; Genesis 35:23–26"> Now Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; kept the flock of Yitro &#91;Excellence&#93; his father in law, the priest of Midyan &#91;Strife&#93;: and he led the flock to the backside of the desert, and came to the mountain of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, &#91;even&#93; to Horev &#91;Desert&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 35:22"> And the angel of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) appeared unto him in a flame of fire out of the midst of a bush: and he looked, and, behold, the bush burned with fire, and the bush &#91;was&#93; not consumed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 28:20; Genesis 35:23"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said, I will now turn aside, and see this great sight, why the bush is not burnt.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> And when the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) saw that he turned aside to see, God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word&#93; called unto him out of the midst of the bush, and said, Moshe, Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;. And he said, Here &#91;am&#93; I.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Genesis 46:26–27; Judges 8:30"> And he said, Draw not near here: put off youi shoes from off youi feet, for the place whereon youi stand &#91;is&#93; holy ground.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 50:26; Genesis 50:24; Acts 7:14–16"> Moreover he said, I &#91;am&#93; the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of youri father, the God-Elōhīm &#91;The Powerful Living Word&#93; of Avraham &#91;Exalted Father&#93;, the God-Elōhīm &#91;The Powerful One&#93; of Yitz’chak &#91;Laughter&#93;, and the God-Elōhīm &#91;The Powerful Creator&#93; of Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;. And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; hid his face; for he was afraid to look upon God-Elōhīm &#91;The Living Word&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Genesis 46:3; Genesis 1:28; Deuteronomy 26:5"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said, I have surely seen the affliction of my people which &#91;are&#93; in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, and have heard their cry by reason of their taskmasters; for I know their sorrows;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Acts 7:18; Ecclesiastes 2:18–19; Ecclesiastes 9:15"> And I am come down to deliver them out of the hand of the Mitzri, and to bring them up out of that land unto a good land and a large, unto a land flowing with milk and honey; unto the place of the Kena’ani &#91;Merchant&#93;, and the Hitti &#91;Terrorists&#93;, and the Emori &#91;Highlanders&#93;, and the P’rizi &#91;Dwellers in Unwalled Towns&#93;, and the Hivi &#91;Villagers&#93;, and the Y’vusi &#91;Threshers, Trampled Under Foot&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24–25; Ecclesiastes 4:4; Jacob 3:14–16"> Now therefore, behold, the cry of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; is come unto me: and I have also seen the oppression which the Mitzri oppress them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Tehillim - Psalms 83:3–4; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:11"> Come now therefore, and I will send youi unto Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93;, that youi may bring forth my people the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 15:13; Genesis 47:11; Exodus 3:7"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, Who &#91;am&#93; I, that I should go unto Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93;, and that I should bring forth the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> And he said, Certainly, I will be with youi; and this &#91;shall be&#93; a token unto youi, that I have sent youi: When youi have brought forth the people out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, you2f shall serve God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; upon this mountain.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:20"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, Behold, &#91;when&#93; I come unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and shall say unto them, The God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of your fathers has sent me unto you; and they shall say to me, What &#91;is&#93; his name? what shall I say unto them?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Numbers 20:15; Exodus 2:23"> And God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;: Ehyeh Asher Ehyeh &#91;I am/will be what I am/will be&#93; and he said, “Thus shall youi say unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: ‘Ehyeh &#91;I Am or I Will Be&#93; …has sent me unto you.”</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; said moreover unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, Thus shall youi say unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: &#91;The LORD-Yehōvah&#93; of your fathers, the God-Elōhīm &#91;Creator&#93; of Avraham &#91;Exalted Father&#93;, the God-Elōhīm &#91;Creator&#93; of Yitz’chak &#91;Laughter&#93;, and the God-Elōhīm &#91;Creator&#93; of Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;, has sent me unto you: this &#91;is&#93; my name forever, and this &#91;is&#93; my memorial unto all generations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Exodus 1:22; Gosel 21:38; Revelation 12:4"> Go, and gather the elders of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; together, and say unto them, The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of your fathers, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Avraham &#91;Exalted Father&#93;, of Yitz’chak &#91;Laughter&#93;, and of Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;, appeared unto me, saying, I have surely visited you, and &#91;seen&#93; that which is done to you in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; went out from Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93;: and Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; cried unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) because of the frogs which he had brought against Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, Stretch out youi hand over the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; for the locusts, that they may come up upon the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, and eat every herb of the land, &#91;even&#93; all that the hail has left.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:20"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; stretched forth his rod over the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) brought an east wind upon the land all that day, and all &#91;that&#93; night; &#91;and&#93; when it was morning, the east wind brought the locusts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Numbers 20:15; Exodus 2:23"> And the locusts went up over all the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, and rested in all the coasts of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;: very grievous &#91;were they&#93;; before them there were no such locusts as they, neither after them shall be such.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> For they covered the face of the whole earth, so that the land was darkened; and they did eat every herb of the land, and all the fruit of the trees which the hail had left: and there remained not any green thing in the trees, or in the herbs of the field, through all the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Exodus 1:22; Gosel 21:38; Revelation 12:4"> Then Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; called for Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; in haste; and he said, I have sinned against the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and against you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 5:29; Exodus 1:21; Gosel 10:28"> Now therefore forgive, I pray youi, my sin only this once, and intreat the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, that he may take away from me this death only.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 8:4; 2 Samuel 13:28"> And he went out from Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93;, and entreated the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 2:4–24; 2 Samuel 17:19–20; 1 Samuel 21:2"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) turned a mighty strong west wind, which took away the locusts, and cast them into the Red sea; there remained not one locust in all the coasts of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Isaiah 3:10; Ecclesiastes 8:12; Mishlei - Proverbs 11:18"> But the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) hardened Par’oh’s &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; heart, so that he would not let the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; go.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="1 Kings 11:38; 1 Samuel 2:35; Tehillim - Psalms 37:3"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, Stretch out youi hand toward heaven, that there may be darkness over the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, even darkness &#91;which&#93; may be felt.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:16; Exodus 1:16"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; stretched forth his hand toward heaven; and there was a thick darkness in all the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; three days:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> They saw not one another, neither rose any from his place for three days: but all the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; had light in their dwellings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; called unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, and said, Go you2f, serve the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); only let your flocks and your herds be detained (held): let your little ones also go with you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said, youi must give us also sacrifices and burnt offerings, that we may sacrifice unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Our cattle also shall go with us; there shall not an hoof be left behind; for thereof must we take to serve the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;; and we know not with what we must serve the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), until we come there.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> But the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) hardened Par’oh’s &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; heart, and he would not let them go.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> And Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; said unto him, Get youi from me, take heed to yourself, see my face no more; for in &#91;that&#93; day youi see my face youi shall die.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said, youi have spoken well, I will see youi face again no more.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 2:1–2; Genesis 46:8–26; Genesis 35:23–26"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, Yet will I bring one plague &#91;more&#93; upon Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93;, and upon Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;; afterwards he will let you go hence: when he shall let &#91;you&#93; go, he shall surely thrust you out hence altogether.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 35:22"> Speak now in the ears of the people, and let every man borrow of his neighbour, and every woman of her neighbour, jewels of silver, and jewels of gold.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 28:20; Genesis 35:23"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) gave the people favour in the sight of the Mitzri. Moreover the man Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; &#91;was&#93; very great in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, in the sight of Par’oh’s &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; servants, and in the sight of the people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said, Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), About midnight will I go out into the midst of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Genesis 46:26–27; Judges 8:30"> And all the firstborn in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; shall die, from the firstborn of Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; that sits upon his throne, even unto the firstborn of the maidservant that &#91;is&#93; behind the mill; and all the firstborn of beasts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 50:26; Genesis 50:24; Acts 7:14–16"> And there shall be a great cry throughout all the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, such as there was none like it, nor shall be like it any more.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Genesis 46:3; Genesis 1:28; Deuteronomy 26:5"> But against any of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; shall not a dog move his tongue, against man or beast: that you2f may know how that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) does put a difference between the Mitzri and Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Acts 7:18; Ecclesiastes 2:18–19; Ecclesiastes 9:15"> And all these youi servants shall come down unto me, and bow down themselves unto me, saying, Get youi out, and all the people that follow youi: and after that I will go out. And he went out from Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; in a great anger.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24–25; Ecclesiastes 4:4; Jacob 3:14–16"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; shall not hearken unto you; that my wonders may be multiplied in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Tehillim - Psalms 83:3–4; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:11"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; did all these wonders before Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93;: and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) hardened Par’oh’s &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; heart, so that he would not let the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; go out of his land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 2:1–2; Genesis 46:8–26; Genesis 35:23–26"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 35:22"> This month &#91;shall be&#93; unto you the beginning of months: it &#91;shall be&#93; the first month of the year to you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 28:20; Genesis 35:23"> Speak you2f unto all the congregation of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, saying, In the tenth &#91;day&#93; of this month they shall take to them every man a lamb, according to the house of &#91;their&#93; fathers, a lamb for an house:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> And if the household be too little for the lamb, let him and his neighbour next unto his house take &#91;it&#93; according to the number of the souls; every man according to his eating shall make your count for the lamb.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Genesis 46:26–27; Judges 8:30"> Your lamb shall be without blemish, a male of the first year: you2f shall take &#91;it&#93; out from the sheep, or from the goats:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 50:26; Genesis 50:24; Acts 7:14–16"> And you2f shall keep it up until the fourteenth day of the same month: and the whole assembly of the congregation of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; shall kill it in the evening.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Genesis 46:3; Genesis 1:28; Deuteronomy 26:5"> And they shall take of the blood, and strike &#91;it&#93; on the two side posts and on the upper doorpost of the houses, wherein they shall eat it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Acts 7:18; Ecclesiastes 2:18–19; Ecclesiastes 9:15"> And they shall eat the flesh in that night, roast with fire, and unleavened bread; &#91;and&#93; with bitter &#91;herbs&#93; they shall eat it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24–25; Ecclesiastes 4:4; Jacob 3:14–16"> Eat not of it raw, nor coocked (boiled) at all with water, but roast &#91;with&#93; fire; his head with his legs, and with the pertinence thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Tehillim - Psalms 83:3–4; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:11"> And you2f shall let nothing of it remain until the morning; and that which remains of it until the morning you2f shall burn with fire.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 15:13; Genesis 47:11; Exodus 3:7"> And thus shall you2f eat it; &#91;with&#93; your waist girded, your shoes on your feet, and your staff in your hand; and you2f shall eat it in haste: it &#91;is&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate’s&#93; Passover.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> For I will pass through the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; this night, and will strike all the firstborn in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, both man and beast; and against all the gods of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; I will execute judgment: I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:20"> And the blood shall be to you for a token upon the houses where you2f &#91;are&#93;: and when I see the blood, I will pass over you, and the plague shall not be upon you to destroy &#91;you&#93;, when I strike the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Numbers 20:15; Exodus 2:23"> And this day shall be unto you for a memorial; and you2f shall keep it a feast to the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) throughout your generations; you2f shall keep it a feast by an ordinance forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> Seven days shall you2f eat unleavened bread; even the first day you2f shall put away leaven out of your houses: for whosoever eats leavened bread from the first day until the seventh day, that soul shall be cut off from Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Exodus 1:22; Gosel 21:38; Revelation 12:4"> And in the first day &#91;there shall be&#93; an holy convocation, and in the seventh day there shall be an holy convocation to you; no manner of work shall be done in them, save &#91;that&#93; which every man must eat, that only may be done of you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 5:29; Exodus 1:21; Gosel 10:28"> And you2f shall observe &#91;the feast of&#93; unleavened bread; for in this selfsame day have I brought your armies out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;: therefore shall you2f observe this day in your generations by an ordinance forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 8:4; 2 Samuel 13:28"> In the first &#91;month&#93;, on the fourteenth day of the month at even, you2f shall eat unleavened bread, until the one and twentieth day of the month at even.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 2:4–24; 2 Samuel 17:19–20; 1 Samuel 21:2"> Seven days shall there be no leaven found in your houses: for whosoever eats that which is leavened, even that soul shall be cut off from the congregation of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, whether he be a stranger, or born in the land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Isaiah 3:10; Ecclesiastes 8:12; Mishlei - Proverbs 11:18"> You2f shall eat nothing leavened; in all your habitations shall you2f eat unleavened bread.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="1 Kings 11:38; 1 Samuel 2:35; Tehillim - Psalms 37:3"> Then Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; called for all the elders of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and said unto them, Draw out and take you a lamb according to your families, and kill the Passover.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:16; Exodus 1:16"> And you2f shall take a bunch of hyssop, and dip &#91;it&#93; in the blood that &#91;is&#93; in the basin, and strike the lintel (top of the door frame) and the two side posts with the blood that &#91;is&#93; in the basin; and none of you shall go out at the door of his house until the morning.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> For the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) will pass through to strike the Mitzri; and when he sees the blood upon the lintel (top of the door frame), and on the two side posts, the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) will pass over the door, and will not suffer the destroyer to come in unto your houses to strike &#91;you&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And you2f shall observe this thing for an ordinance to youi and to youi sons forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And it shall come to pass, when you2f be come to the land which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) will give you, according as he has promised, that you2f shall keep this service.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> And it shall come to pass, when your children shall say unto you, What mean you2f by this service?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> That you2f shall say, It &#91;is&#93; the sacrifice of the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; Passover, who passed over the houses of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, when he struck the Mitzri, and delivered our houses. And the people bowed the head and worshipped.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> And the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; went away, and did as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, so did they.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And it came to pass, that at midnight the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) struck all the firstborn in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, from the firstborn of Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; that sat on his throne unto the firstborn of the captive that &#91;was&#93; in the dungeon; and all the firstborn of cattle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> And Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; rose up in the night, he, and all his servants, and all the Mitzri; and there was a great cry in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;; for &#91;there was&#93; not a house where &#91;there was&#93; not one dead.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And he called for Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; by night, and said, Rise up, &#91;and&#93; get you forth from among my people, both you2f and the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; and go, serve the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), as you2f have said.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> Also take your flocks and your herds, as you2f have said, and be gone; and bless me also.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And the Mitzri were urgent upon the people, that they might send them out of the land in haste; for they said, We &#91;be&#93; all dead &#91;men&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> And the people took their dough before it was leavened, their kneading troughs being bound up in their clothes upon their shoulders.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; did according to the word of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;; and they borrowed of the Mitzri jewels of silver, and jewels of gold, and clothing:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) gave the people favour in the sight of the Mitzri, so that they lent unto them &#91;such things as they required&#93;. And they spoiled the Mitzri.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> And the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; journeyed from Ra’amses &#91;Child of the Sun God&#93; to Sukkot &#91;Booths&#93; &#91;Tabernacles/Booths&#93;, about six hundred thousand on foot &#91;that were&#93; men, beside children.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> And a mixed multitude went up also with them; and flocks, and herds, &#91;even&#93; very much cattle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> And they baked unleavened cakes of the dough which they brought forth out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, for it was not leavened; because they were thrust out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, and could not wait, neither had they prepared for themselves any victual.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> Now the sojourning of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, who dwelt in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, &#91;was&#93; four hundred and thirty years.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> And it came to pass at the end of the four hundred and thirty years, even the selfsame day it came to pass, that all the hosts of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) went out from the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> It &#91;is&#93; a night to be much observed unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) for bringing them out from the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;: this &#91;is&#93; that night of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) to be observed of all the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; in their generations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, This &#91;is&#93; the ordinance of the Passover: There shall no stranger eat thereof:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> But every man’s servant that is bought for money, when youi have circumcised him, then shall he eat thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45"> A foreigner and an hired servant shall not eat thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46"> In one house shall it be eaten; youi shall not carry forth ought of the flesh abroad out of the house; neither shall you2f break a bone thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47"> All the congregation of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; shall keep it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48"> And when a stranger shall sojourn with youi, and will keep the Passover to the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), let all his males be circumcised, and then let him come near and keep it; and he shall be as one that is born in the land: for no uncircumcised person shall eat thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49"> One law shall be to him that is homeborn, and unto the stranger that sojourners among you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50"> Thus did all the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, so did they.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v51" data-v="51"> And it came to pass the selfsame day, &#91;that&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) did bring the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; by their armies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 2:1–2; Genesis 46:8–26; Genesis 35:23–26"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 35:22"> Sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) unto me all the firstborn, whatsoever opens the womb among the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, &#91;both&#93; of man and of beast: it &#91;is&#93; my.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 28:20; Genesis 35:23"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto the people, Remember this day, in which you2f came out from Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, out of the house of bondage; for by strength of hand the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) brought you out from this &#91;place&#93;: there shall no leavened bread be eaten.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> This day came you2f out in the month Aviv.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Genesis 46:26–27; Judges 8:30"> And it shall be when the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) shall bring youi into the land of the Kena’ani &#91;Merchant&#93;, and the Hitti &#91;Terrorists&#93;, and the Emori &#91;Highlanders&#93;, and the Hivi &#91;Villagers&#93;, and the Y’vusi &#91;Threshers, Trampled Under Foot&#93;, which he swore unto youi fathers to give youi, a land flowing with milk and honey, that youi shall keep this service in this month.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 50:26; Genesis 50:24; Acts 7:14–16"> Seven days youi shall eat unleavened bread, and in the seventh day &#91;shall be&#93; a feast to the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Genesis 46:3; Genesis 1:28; Deuteronomy 26:5"> Unleavened bread shall be eaten seven days; and there shall no leavened bread be seen with youi, neither shall there be leaven seen with youi in all youi quarters.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Acts 7:18; Ecclesiastes 2:18–19; Ecclesiastes 9:15"> And youi shall show youi son in that day, saying, &#91;This is done&#93; because of that &#91;which&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) did unto me when I came forth out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24–25; Ecclesiastes 4:4; Jacob 3:14–16"> And it shall be for a sign unto youi upon youi hand, and for a memorial between youi eyes, that the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; law may be in youi mouth: for with a strong hand has the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) brought youi out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> That youi shall set apart unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) all that opens the womb, and every firstling that comes of a beast which youi have; the males &#91;shall be&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:20"> And every firstling of an ass youi shall redeem with a lamb; and if youi will not redeem it, then youi shall break his neck: and all the firstborn of man among youi children shall youi redeem.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Numbers 20:15; Exodus 2:23"> And it shall be when youri son asks youi in time to come, saying, What &#91;is&#93; this? that youi shall say unto him, By strength of hand the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) brought us out from Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, from the house of bondage:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And it came to pass, when Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; would hardly let us go, that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) killed all the firstborn in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, both the firstborn of man, and the firstborn of beast: therefore I sacrifice to the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) all that opens the womb, being males; but all the firstborn of my children I redeem.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Exodus 1:22; Gosel 21:38; Revelation 12:4"> And it shall be for a token upon youi hand, and for frontlets between youi eyes: for by strength of hand the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) brought us forth out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 5:29; Exodus 1:21; Gosel 10:28"> And it came to pass, when Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; had let the people go, that God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; led them not &#91;through&#93; the way of the land of the P’lishtim &#91;Immigrants&#93;, although that &#91;was&#93; near; for God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; said, Lest perhaps the people repent when they see war, and they return to Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;:</span>
</div>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 2:4–24; 2 Samuel 17:19–20; 1 Samuel 21:2"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; took the bones of Yosef &#91;(God) He Shall Add&#93; with him: for he had straightly sworn the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, saying, God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; will surely visit you; and you2f shall carry up my bones away hence with you.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 35:22"> Speak unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, that they turn and encamp before Pi-Hachirot &#91;The Place where the Sedge Grows&#93;, between Migdol &#91;Tower&#93; and the sea, over against Ba’al-Tz’fon &#91;Lord of the North&#93;: before it shall you2f encamp by the sea.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> And I will harden Par’oh’s &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; heart, that he shall follow after them; and I will be honoured upon Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93;, and upon all his host; that the Mitzri may know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate). And they did so.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Genesis 46:26–27; Judges 8:30"> And it was told the king of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; that the people fled: and the heart of Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; and of his servants was turned against the people, and they said, Why have we done this, that we have let Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; go from serving us?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Acts 7:18; Ecclesiastes 2:18–19; Ecclesiastes 9:15"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) hardened the heart of Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; king of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, and he pursued after the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: and the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; went out with an high hand.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24–25; Ecclesiastes 4:4; Jacob 3:14–16"> But the Mitzri pursued after them, all the horses &#91;and&#93; chariots of Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93;, and his horsemen, and his army, and overtook them encamping by the sea, beside Pi-Hachirot &#91;The Place where the Sedge Grows&#93;, before Ba’al-Tz’fon &#91;Lord of the North&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Tehillim - Psalms 83:3–4; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:11"> And when Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; drew near, the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; lifted up their eyes, and, behold, the Mitzri marched after them; and they were sore afraid: and the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; cried out unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 15:13; Genesis 47:11; Exodus 3:7"> And they said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, Because &#91;there were&#93; no graves in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, have youi taken us away to die in the wilderness? wherefore have youi dealt thus with us, to carry us forth out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;?</span>

<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> &#91;Is&#93; not this the word that we did tell youi in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, saying, Let us alone, that we may serve the Mitzri? For &#91;it had been&#93; better for us to serve the Mitzri, than that we should die in the wilderness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:20"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto the people, Fear you2f not, stand still, and see the salvation of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), which he will show to you today: for the Mitzri whom you2f have seen today, you2f shall see them again no more forever.</span>

<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Numbers 20:15; Exodus 2:23"> The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) shall fight for you, and you2f shall hold your peace.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, For what reason do youi cry unto me? speak unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, that they go forward:</span>

<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Exodus 1:22; Gosel 21:38; Revelation 12:4"> But lift youi up youi rod, and stretch out youi hand over the sea, and divide it: and the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; shall go on dry &#91;ground&#93; through the midst of the sea.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 5:29; Exodus 1:21; Gosel 10:28"> And I, behold, I will harden the hearts of the Mitzri, and they shall follow them: and I will get me honour upon Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93;, and upon all his host, upon his chariots, and upon his horsemen.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 2:4–24; 2 Samuel 17:19–20; 1 Samuel 21:2"> And the angel of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, which went before the camp of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, removed and went behind them; and the pillar of the cloud went from before their face, and stood behind them:</span>

</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And took off their chariot wheels, that they drove them heavily: so that the Mitzri said, Let us flee from the face of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) fights for them against the Mitzri.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, Stretch out youi hand over the sea, that the waters may come again upon the Mitzri, upon their chariots, and upon their horsemen.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; stretched forth his hand over the sea, and the sea returned to his strength when the morning appeared; and the Mitzri fled against it; and the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) overthrew the Mitzri in the midst of the sea.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> And the waters returned, and covered the chariots, and the horsemen, &#91;and&#93; all the host of Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; that came into the sea after them; there remained not so much as one of them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> But the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; walked upon dry &#91;land&#93; in the midst of the sea; and the waters &#91;were&#93; a wall unto them on their right hand, and on their left.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> Thus the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) saved Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; that day out of the hand of the Mitzri; and Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; saw the Mitzri dead upon the sea shore.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; saw that great work which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) did upon the Mitzri: and the people feared the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and believed the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and his servant Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 2:1–2; Genesis 46:8–26; Genesis 35:23–26"> Then sang Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; this song unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and spoke, saying, I will sing unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), for he has triumphed gloriously: the horse and his rider has he thrown into the sea.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 35:22"> The LORD-Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;is&#93; my strength and song, and he is become my salvation: he &#91;is&#93; my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and I will prepare him an habitation; my father’s God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and I will exalt him.</span>

<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 28:20; Genesis 35:23"> The LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;is&#93; a man of war: the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;is&#93; his name.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> Par’oh’s &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; chariots and his host has he cast into the sea: his chosen captains also are drowned in the Red sea.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Genesis 46:26–27; Judges 8:30"> The depths have covered them: they sank into the bottom as a stone.</span>

<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 15:13; Genesis 47:11; Exodus 3:7"> Who &#91;is&#93; like unto youi, O LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), among the gods? who &#91;is&#93; like youi, glorious in holiness, fearful &#91;in&#93; praises, doing wonders?</span>

</div>

</div>

<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="1 Kings 11:38; 1 Samuel 2:35; Tehillim - Psalms 37:3"> And Miryam &#91;rebellion&#93; answered them, Sing you2f to the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), for he has triumphed gloriously; the horse and his rider has he thrown into the sea.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And when they came to Marah &#91;Bitterness&#93;, they could not drink of the waters of Marah &#91;Bitterness&#93;, for they &#91;were&#93; bitter: therefore the name of it was called Marah &#91;Bitterness&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And the people murmured against Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying, What shall we drink?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 28:20; Genesis 35:23"> And the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; said unto them, Would to God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; we had died by the hand of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, when we sat by the flesh pots, &#91;and&#93; when we did eat bread to the full; for you2f have brought us forth into this wilderness, to kill this whole assembly with hunger.</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> I have heard the murmurings of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: speak unto them, saying, At even you2f shall eat flesh, and in the morning you2f shall be filled with bread; and you2f shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>

And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto them, This &#91;is&#93; the bread which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has given you to eat.</span>

</div>

<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 8:4; 2 Samuel 13:28"> And when they did measure (deal out) &#91;it&#93; with an omer, he that gathered much had nothing over, and he that gathered little had no lack; they gathered every man according to his eating.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:16; Exodus 1:16"> And it came to pass, &#91;that&#93; on the sixth day they gathered twice as much bread, two omers for one &#91;man&#93;: and all the rulers of the congregation came and told Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And they laid it up till the morning, as Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; bade: and it did not stink, neither was there any worm in it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said, Eat that today; for today &#91;is&#93; a Shabbat &#91;Rest&#93; unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): today you2f shall not find it in the field.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Six days you2f shall gather it; but on the seventh day, &#91;which is&#93; the Shabbat &#91;Rest&#93;, in it there shall be none.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> And it came to pass, &#91;that&#93; there went out &#91;some&#93; of the people on the seventh day for to gather, and they found none.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, How long refuse you2f to keep my commandments and my laws?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> See, for that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has given you the Shabbat &#91;Rest&#93;, therefore he gives you on the sixth day the bread of two days; abide you2f every man in his place, let no man go out of his place on the seventh day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; called the name thereof Manna: and it &#91;was&#93; like coriander seed, white; and the taste of it &#91;was&#93; like wafers &#91;made&#93; with honey.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said, This &#91;is&#93; the thing which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commands, Fill an omer of it to be kept for your generations; that they may see the bread which I have fed you in the wilderness, when I brought you forth from the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, Take a pot, and put an omer full of manna in it, and lay it up before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), to be kept for your generations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> As the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, so Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; laid it up before the Testimony, to be kept.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 2:1–2; Genesis 46:8–26; Genesis 35:23–26"> And all the congregation of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; journeyed from the wilderness of Sin, after their journeys, according to the commandment of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and pitched in Refidim &#91;Resting Places&#93;: and &#91;there was&#93; no water for the people to drink.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto them, Why quarrel you2f with me? wherefore do you2f tempt the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate)?</span>

<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 28:20; Genesis 35:23"> And the people thirsted there for water; and the people murmured against Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, and said, For what reason &#91;is&#93; this &#91;that&#93; youi have brought us up out of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, to kill us and our children and our cattle with thirst?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; cried unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), saying, What shall I do unto this people? they be almost ready to stone me.</span>

<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Genesis 46:26–27; Judges 8:30"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, Go on before the people, and take with youi of the elders of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; and youri rod, which youi stuck the river, take in youri hand, and go.</span>

<div className="bp">

And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; did so in the sight of the elders of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Genesis 46:3; Genesis 1:28; Deuteronomy 26:5"> And he called the name of the place Massah &#91;Testing&#93;, and M’rivah &#91;Arguing&#93;, because of the chiding of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and because they tempted the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), saying, Is the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) among us, or not?</span>

<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Acts 7:18; Ecclesiastes 2:18–19; Ecclesiastes 9:15"> Then came ‘Amalek &#91;Dweller in a Valley&#93;, and fought with Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; in Refidim &#91;Resting Places&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24–25; Ecclesiastes 4:4; Jacob 3:14–16"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto Y’hoshua &#91;Yehovah is Salvation&#93;, Choose us out men, and go out, fight with ‘Amalek &#91;Dweller in a Valley&#93;: tomorrow I will stand on the top of the hill with the rod of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; in my hand.</span>

<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Tehillim - Psalms 83:3–4; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:11"> So Y’hoshua &#91;Yehovah is Salvation&#93; did as Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; had said to him, and fought with ‘Amalek &#91;Dweller in a Valley&#93;: and Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, and Hur &#91;Liberty&#93; went up to the top of the hill.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 15:13; Genesis 47:11; Exodus 3:7"> And it came to pass, when Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; held up his hand, that Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; prevailed: and when he let down his hand, ‘Amalek &#91;Dweller in a Valley&#93; prevailed.</span>
