</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Tehillim - Psalms 83:3–4; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:11"> But the seventh day &#91;is&#93; the Shabbat &#91;Rest&#93; of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) youi God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: &#91;in it&#93; youi shall not do any work, youi, nor youi son, nor youi daughter, youi manservant, nor youi maidservant, nor youi cattle, nor youi stranger that &#91;is&#93; within youi gates:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 15:13; Genesis 47:11; Exodus 3:7"> For &#91;in&#93; six days the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) made heaven and earth, the sea, and all that in them &#91;is&#93;, and rested the seventh day: wherefore the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) the Shabbat &#91;Rest&#93; day, and consecrated it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> Honour youi father and youi mother: that youi days may be long upon the land which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) youi God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; gives youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:20"> youi shall not kill.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Numbers 20:15; Exodus 2:23"> youi shall not commit adultery.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> youi shall not steal.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Exodus 1:22; Gosel 21:38; Revelation 12:4"> youi shall not bear false witness against youi neighbour.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 5:29; Exodus 1:21; Gosel 10:28"> youi shall not covet youi neighbour’s house, youi shall not covet youri neighbour’s wife, nor his manservant, nor his maidservant, nor his ox, nor his ass, nor anything that &#91;is&#93; youri neighbour’s.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 8:4; 2 Samuel 13:28"> And all the people saw the thunderings, and the lightnings, and the noise of the trumpet, and the mountain smoking: and when the people saw &#91;it&#93;, they removed, and stood afar off.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 2:4–24; 2 Samuel 17:19–20; 1 Samuel 21:2"> And they said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, Speak youi with us, and we will hear: but let not God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; speak with us, lest we die.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Isaiah 3:10; Ecclesiastes 8:12; Mishlei - Proverbs 11:18"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto the people, Fear not: for God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; is come to prove you, and that his fear may be before your faces, that you2f sin not.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="1 Kings 11:38; 1 Samuel 2:35; Tehillim - Psalms 37:3"> And the people stood afar off, and Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; drew near unto the thick darkness where God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;was&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:16; Exodus 1:16"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, Thus youi shall say unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, You2f have seen that I have talked with you from heaven.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> You2f shall not make with me gods of silver, neither shall you2f make unto you gods of gold.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> An altar of earth youi shall make unto me, and shall sacrifice thereon youi burnt offerings, and youi peace offerings, youi sheep, and youi oxen: in all places where I record my name I will come unto youi, and I will bless youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And if youi will make me an altar of stone, youi shall not build it of cut stone: for if youi lift up youi tool upon it, youi have polluted it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Neither shall youi go up by steps unto my altar, that youi nakedness be not discovered thereon.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 2:1–2; Genesis 46:8–26; Genesis 35:23–26"> Now these &#91;are&#93; the judgments which youi shall set before them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 35:22"> If youi buy an ‘Evri’ot &#91;Hebrew&#93; servant, six years he shall serve: and in the seventh he shall go out free for nothing.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 28:20; Genesis 35:23"> If he came in by himself, he shall go out by himself: if he were married, then his wife shall go out with him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> If his master have given him a wife, and she have born him sons or daughters; the wife and her children shall be her master’s, and he shall go out by himself.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Genesis 46:26–27; Judges 8:30"> And if the servant shall plainly say, I love my master, my wife, and my children; I will not go out free:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 50:26; Genesis 50:24; Acts 7:14–16"> Then his master shall bring him unto the judges; he shall also bring him to the door, or unto the door post; and his master shall bore his ear through with an aul; and he shall serve him forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Genesis 46:3; Genesis 1:28; Deuteronomy 26:5"> And if a man sell his daughter to be a maidservant, she shall not go out as the menservants do.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Acts 7:18; Ecclesiastes 2:18–19; Ecclesiastes 9:15"> If she please not her master, who has betrothed her to himself, then shall he let her be redeemed: to sell her unto a strange nation he shall have no power, seeing he has dealt deceitfully with her.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24–25; Ecclesiastes 4:4; Jacob 3:14–16"> And if he have betrothed her unto his son, he shall deal with her after the manner of daughters.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Tehillim - Psalms 83:3–4; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:11"> If he take him another &#91;wife&#93;; her food, her clothing, and her duty of marriage, shall he not diminish.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 15:13; Genesis 47:11; Exodus 3:7"> And if he do not these three unto her, then shall she go out free without money.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> He that smites a man, so that he die, shall be surely put to death.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:20"> And if a man lie not in wait, but God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; deliver &#91;him&#93; into his hand; then I will appoint youi a place to which he shall flee.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Numbers 20:15; Exodus 2:23"> But if a man come presumptuously upon his neighbour, to slay him with deceit; youi shall take him from my altar, that he may die.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And he that smites his father, or his mother, shall be surely put to death.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Exodus 1:22; Gosel 21:38; Revelation 12:4"> And he that steals a man, and sells him, or if he be found in his hand, he shall surely be put to death.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 5:29; Exodus 1:21; Gosel 10:28"> And he that curses his father, or his mother, shall surely be put to death.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 8:4; 2 Samuel 13:28"> And if men strive together, and one strike another with a stone, or with &#91;his&#93; fist, and he die not, but keeps &#91;his&#93; bed:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 2:4–24; 2 Samuel 17:19–20; 1 Samuel 21:2"> If he rise again, and walk abroad upon his staff, then shall he that struck &#91;him&#93; be quit (acquitted; found not guilty): only he shall pay &#91;for&#93; the loss of his time, and shall cause &#91;him&#93; to be thoroughly healed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Isaiah 3:10; Ecclesiastes 8:12; Mishlei - Proverbs 11:18"> And if a man strike his servant, or his maid, with a rod, and he die under his hand; he shall be surely punished.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="1 Kings 11:38; 1 Samuel 2:35; Tehillim - Psalms 37:3"> Yet, if he continue a day or two, he shall not be punished: for he &#91;is&#93; his money.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:16; Exodus 1:16"> If men strive, and hurt a woman with child, so that her fruit depart &#91;from her&#93;, and yet no mischief follow: he shall be surely punished, according as the woman’s husband will lay upon him; and he shall pay as the &#91;determine&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And if &#91;any&#93; mischief follow, then youi shall give life for life,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> Eye for eye, tooth for tooth, hand for hand, foot for foot,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> Burning for burning, wound for wound, stripe for stripe.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> And if a man strike the eye of his servant, or the eye of his maid, that it perish; he shall let him go free for his eye’s sake.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> And if he strike out his manservant’s tooth, or his maidservant’s tooth; he shall let him go free for his tooth’s sake.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> If an ox gore a man or a woman, that they die: then the ox shall be surely stoned, and his flesh shall not be eaten; but the owner of the ox &#91;shall be&#93; quit (acquitted; found not guilty).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> But if the ox were wont (used to; in the habit of) to push with his horn in time past, and it has been testified to his owner, and he has not kept him in, but that he has killed a man or a woman; the ox shall be stoned, and his owner also shall be put to death.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> If there be laid on him a sum of money, then he shall give for the ransom of his life whatsoever is laid upon him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> Whether he have gored a son, or have gored a daughter, according to this judgment shall it be done unto him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> If the ox shall push a manservant or a maidservant; he shall give unto their master thirty shekels of silver, and the ox shall be stoned.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And if a man shall open a pit, or if a man shall dig a pit, and not cover it, and an ox or an ass fall in it;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> The owner of the pit shall make &#91;it&#93; good, &#91;and&#93; give money unto the owner of them; and the dead &#91;beast&#93; shall be his.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And if one man’s ox hurt another’s, that he die; then they shall sell the live ox, and divide the money of it; and the dead &#91;ox&#93; also they shall divide.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> Or if it be known that the ox has used to push in time past, and his owner has not kept him in; he shall surely pay ox for ox; and the dead shall be his own.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 2:1–2; Genesis 46:8–26; Genesis 35:23–26"> If a man shall steal an ox, or a sheep, and kill it, or sell it; he shall restore five oxen for an ox, and four sheep for a sheep.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 35:22"> If a thief be found breaking up, and be smitten that he die, &#91;there shall&#93; no blood &#91;be shed&#93; for him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 28:20; Genesis 35:23"> If the sun be risen upon him, &#91;there shall be&#93; blood &#91;shed&#93; for him; &#91;for&#93; he should make full restitution; if he have nothing, then he shall be sold for his theft.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> If the theft be certainly found in his hand alive, whether it be ox, or ass, or sheep; he shall restore double.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Genesis 46:26–27; Judges 8:30"> If a man shall cause a field or vineyard to be eaten, and shall put in his beast, and shall feed in another man’s field; of the best of his own field, and of the best of his own vineyard, shall he make restitution.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 50:26; Genesis 50:24; Acts 7:14–16"> If fire break out, and catch in thorns, so that the stacks of corn, or the standing corn, or the field, be consumed &#91;with that&#93;; he that kindled the fire shall surely make restitution.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Genesis 46:3; Genesis 1:28; Deuteronomy 26:5"> If a man shall deliver unto his neighbour money or stuff to keep, and it be stolen out of the man’s house; if the thief be found, let him pay double.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Acts 7:18; Ecclesiastes 2:18–19; Ecclesiastes 9:15"> If the thief be not found, then the master of the house shall be brought unto the judges, &#91;to see&#93; whether he have put his hand unto his neighbour’s goods.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24–25; Ecclesiastes 4:4; Jacob 3:14–16"> For all manner of trespass, &#91;whether it be&#93; for ox, for ass, for sheep, for clothing, &#91;or&#93; for any manner of lost thing, which &#91;another&#93; challenges to be his, the cause of both parties shall come before the judges; &#91;and&#93; whom the shall condemn, he shall pay double unto his neighbour.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Tehillim - Psalms 83:3–4; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:11"> If a man deliver unto his neighbour an ass, or an ox, or a sheep, or any beast, to keep; and it die, or be hurt, or driven away, no man seeing &#91;it&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 15:13; Genesis 47:11; Exodus 3:7"> &#91;Then&#93; shall an oath of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) be between them both, that he has not put his hand unto his neighbour’s goods; and the owner of it shall accept &#91;thereof&#93;, and he shall not make &#91;it&#93; good.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> And if it be stolen from him, he shall make restitution unto the owner thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:20"> If it be torn in pieces, &#91;then&#93; let him bring it &#91;for&#93; witness, &#91;and&#93; he shall not make good that which was torn.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Numbers 20:15; Exodus 2:23"> And if a man borrow &#91;ought&#93; of his neighbour, and it be hurt, or die, the owner thereof &#91;being&#93; not with it, he shall surely make &#91;it&#93; good.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> &#91;But&#93; if the owner thereof &#91;be&#93; with it, he shall not make &#91;it&#93; good: if it &#91;be&#93; an hired &#91;thing&#93;, it came for his hire.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Exodus 1:22; Gosel 21:38; Revelation 12:4"> And if a man entice a maid that is not betrothed, and lie with her, he shall surely endow her to be his wife.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 5:29; Exodus 1:21; Gosel 10:28"> If her father utterly refuse to give her unto him, he shall pay money according to the dowry of virgins.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 8:4; 2 Samuel 13:28"> youi shall not suffer a witch to live.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 2:4–24; 2 Samuel 17:19–20; 1 Samuel 21:2"> Whosoever lies with a beast shall surely be put to death.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Isaiah 3:10; Ecclesiastes 8:12; Mishlei - Proverbs 11:18"> He that sacrifices unto &#91;any&#93; god, save unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) only, he shall be utterly destroyed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="1 Kings 11:38; 1 Samuel 2:35; Tehillim - Psalms 37:3"> youi shall neither vex a stranger, nor oppress him: for you2f were strangers in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:16; Exodus 1:16"> You2f shall not afflict any widow, or fatherless child.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> If youi afflict them in any wise, and they cry at all unto me, I will surely hear their cry;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And my wrath shall grow (become; advance) hot, and I will kill you with the sword; and your wives shall be widows, and your children fatherless.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> If youi lend money to &#91;any of&#93; my people &#91;that is&#93; poor by youi, youi shall not be to him as an usurer, neither shall youi lay upon him usury (interest paid for using money).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> If youi at all take youi neighbour’s clothing to pledge, youi shall deliver it unto him by that the sun goes down:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> For that &#91;is&#93; his covering only, it &#91;is&#93; his clothing for his skin: wherein shall he sleep? and it shall come to pass, when he cries unto me, that I will hear; for I &#91;am&#93; gracious.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> youi shall not revile the gods, nor curse the ruler of youi people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> youi shall not delay &#91;to offer&#93; the first of youi ripe fruits, and of youi liquors: the firstborn of youi sons shall youi give unto me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> Likewise shall youi do with youi oxen, &#91;and&#93; with youi sheep: seven days it shall be with his mother; on the eighth day youi shall give it me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And you2f shall be holy men unto me: neither shall you2f eat &#91;any&#93; flesh &#91;that is&#93; torn of beasts in the field; you2f shall cast it to the dogs.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 2:1–2; Genesis 46:8–26; Genesis 35:23–26"> youi shall not raise a false report: put not youi hand with the wicked to be an unrighteous witness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 35:22"> youi shall not follow a multitude to &#91;do&#93; evil; neither shall youi speak in a cause to decline after many to wrest &#91;judgment&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 28:20; Genesis 35:23"> Neither shall youi Countenance (Face, appearance; show favor) a poor man in his cause.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> If youi meet youi enemy’s ox or his ass going astray, youi shall surely bring it back to him again.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Genesis 46:26–27; Judges 8:30"> If youi see the ass of him that hates youi lying under his burden, and would refrain (restrain; refrein from acting) to help him, youi shall surely help with him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 50:26; Genesis 50:24; Acts 7:14–16"> youi shall not wrest the judgment of youi poor in his cause.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Genesis 46:3; Genesis 1:28; Deuteronomy 26:5"> Keep youi far from a false matter; and the innocent and righteous slay youi not: for I will not justify the wicked.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Acts 7:18; Ecclesiastes 2:18–19; Ecclesiastes 9:15"> And youi shall take no gift: for the gift blinds the wise, and perverts the words of the righteous.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

In like manner youi shall deal with youi vineyard, &#91;and&#93; with youi oliveyard.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> For my Angel shall go before youi, and bring youi in unto the Emori &#91;Highlanders&#93;, and the Hitti &#91;Terrorists&#93;, and the P’rizi &#91;Dwellers in Unwalled Towns&#93;, and the Kena’ani &#91;Merchant&#93;, the Hivi &#91;Villagers&#93;, and the Y’vusi &#91;Threshers, Trampled Under Foot&#93;: and I will cut them off.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And you2f shall serve the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and he shall bless youi bread, and youi water; and I will take sickness away from the midst of youi.</span>

<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 28:20; Genesis 35:23"> The five curtains shall be coupled together one to another; and &#91;other&#93; five curtains &#91;shall be&#93; coupled one to another.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> And youi shall make loops of blue upon the edge of the one curtain from the selvedge (fabric edge to prevent unraveling) in the coupling; and likewise shall youi make in the uttermost edge of &#91;another&#93; curtain, in the coupling of the second.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Genesis 46:26–27; Judges 8:30"> Fifty loops shall youi make in the one curtain, and fifty loops shall youi make in the edge of the curtain that &#91;is&#93; in the coupling of the second; that the loops may take hold one of another.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 50:26; Genesis 50:24; Acts 7:14–16"> And youi shall make fifty hooks (fasteners) of gold, and couple the curtains together with the hooks (fasteners): and it shall be one tabernacle.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:20"> And a cubit on the one side, and a cubit on the other side of that which remains in the length of the curtains of the tent, it shall hang over the sides of the tabernacle on this side and on that side, to cover it.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Numbers 20:15; Exodus 2:23"> And youi shall make a covering for the tent &#91;of&#93; rams’ skins dyed red, and a covering above &#91;of&#93; badgers’ skins.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And youi shall make boards for the tabernacle &#91;of&#93; shittim wood standing up.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 5:29; Exodus 1:21; Gosel 10:28"> Two tenons &#91;shall there be&#93; in one board, set in order one against another: thus shall youi make for all the boards of the tabernacle.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

</div>

<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And youi shall hang it upon four pillars of shittim &#91;wood&#93; overlaid with gold: their hooks &#91;shall be of&#93; gold, upon the four sockets of silver.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; shall bear the names of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; in the breastplate of judgment upon his heart, when he goes in unto the holy &#91;place&#93;, for a memorial before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) continually.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> And youi shall put in the breastplate of judgment the Urim &#91;Lights&#93; and the Tumim &#91;Perfection&#93;; and they shall be upon Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;’s heart, when he goes in before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; shall bear the judgment of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; upon his heart before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) continually.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And youi shall make the robe of the ephod (vest) all &#91;of&#93; blue.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And there shall be an hole in the top of it, in the midst thereof: it shall have a binding of woven work round about the hole of it, as it were the hole of an habergeon (coat of mail for the neck and down), that it be not rent.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And &#91;beneath&#93; upon the hem of it youi shall make pomegranates &#91;of&#93; blue, and &#91;of&#93; purple, and &#91;of&#93; scarlet, round about the hem thereof; and bells of gold between them round about:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> A golden bell and a pomegranate, a golden bell and a pomegranate, upon the hem of the robe round about.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And it shall be upon Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; to minister: and his sound shall be heard when he goes in unto the holy &#91;place&#93; before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and when he comes out, that he die not.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And youi shall make a plate &#91;of&#93; pure gold, and grave upon it, &#91;like&#93; the engravings of a signet (seal or stamp), HOLINESS TO THE LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> And youi shall put it on a blue lace, that it may be upon the mitre (turban; ceremonial headdress); upon the forefront of the mitre (turban; ceremonial headdress) it shall be.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> And it shall be upon Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;’s forehead, that Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; may bear the iniquity of the holy things, which the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; shall hallow (to approve, commend, accept) in all their holy gifts; and it shall be always upon his forehead, that they may be accepted before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> And youi shall embroider the coat of fine linen, and youi shall make the mitre (turban; ceremonial headdress) &#91;of&#93; fine linen, and youi shall make the belt &#91;of&#93; needlework.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> And for Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;’s sons youi shall make coats, and youi shall make for them girdles, and bonnets shall youi make for them, for glory and for beauty.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> And youi shall put them upon Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; youi brother, and his sons with him; and shall anoint them, and consecrate (treat as set apart or separate unto God) them, and sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) them, that they may minister unto me in the priest’s office.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> And youi shall make them linen trousers to cover their nakedness; from the waist even unto the thighs they shall reach:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> And they shall be upon Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, and upon his sons, when they come in unto the tabernacle of the congregation, or when they come near unto the altar to minister in the holy &#91;place&#93;; that they bear not iniquity, and die: &#91;it shall be&#93; a statute forever unto him and his seed after him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 2:1–2; Genesis 46:8–26; Genesis 35:23–26"> And this &#91;is&#93; the thing that youi shall do unto them to hallow (to approve, commend, accept) them, to minister unto me in the priest’s office: Take one young bullock, and two rams without blemish,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 35:22"> And unleavened bread, and cakes unleavened tempered with oil, and wafers unleavened anointed with oil: &#91;of&#93; wheaten flour shall youi make them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 28:20; Genesis 35:23"> And youi shall put them into one basket, and bring them in the basket, with the bullock and the two rams.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> And Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; and his sons youi shall bring unto the door of the tabernacle of the congregation, and shall wash them with water.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Genesis 46:26–27; Judges 8:30"> And youi shall take the garments, and put upon Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; the coat, and the robe of the ephod (vest), and the ephod (vest), and the breastplate, and gird him with the detailed (skillfully made) belt of the ephod (vest):</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 50:26; Genesis 50:24; Acts 7:14–16"> And youi shall put the mitre (turban; ceremonial headdress) upon his head, and put the holy crown upon the mitre (turban; ceremonial headdress).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Genesis 46:3; Genesis 1:28; Deuteronomy 26:5"> Then shall youi take the anointing oil, and pour &#91;it&#93; upon his head, and anoint him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Acts 7:18; Ecclesiastes 2:18–19; Ecclesiastes 9:15"> And youi shall bring his sons, and put coats upon them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24–25; Ecclesiastes 4:4; Jacob 3:14–16"> And youi shall gird them with girdles, Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; and his sons, and put the bonnets on them: and the priest’s office shall be theirs for a perpetual statute: and youi shall consecrate (treat as set apart or separate unto God) Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; and his sons.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Tehillim - Psalms 83:3–4; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:11"> And youi shall cause a bullock to be brought before the tabernacle of the congregation: and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; and his sons shall put their hands upon the head of the bullock.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 15:13; Genesis 47:11; Exodus 3:7"> And youi shall kill the bullock before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), &#91;by&#93; the door of the tabernacle of the congregation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> And youi shall take of the blood of the bullock, and put &#91;it&#93; upon the horns of the altar with youi finger, and pour all the blood beside the bottom of the altar.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:20"> And youi shall take all the fat that covers the inwards, and the caul (membrane; fatty tissue around a bodily organ) &#91;that is&#93; above the liver, and the two kidneys, and the fat that &#91;is&#93; upon them, and burn &#91;them&#93; upon the altar.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Numbers 20:15; Exodus 2:23"> But the flesh of the bullock, and his skin, and his dung, shall youi burn with fire without the camp: it &#91;is&#93; a sin offering.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> youi shall also take one ram; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; and his sons shall put their hands upon the head of the ram.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Exodus 1:22; Gosel 21:38; Revelation 12:4"> And youi shall slay the ram, and youi shall take his blood, and sprinkle &#91;it&#93; round about upon the altar.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 5:29; Exodus 1:21; Gosel 10:28"> And youi shall cut the ram in pieces, and wash the inwards of him, and his legs, and put &#91;them&#93; unto his pieces, and unto his head.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 8:4; 2 Samuel 13:28"> And youi shall burn the whole ram upon the altar: it &#91;is&#93; a burnt offering unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): it &#91;is&#93; a soothing aroma, an offering made by fire unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 2:4–24; 2 Samuel 17:19–20; 1 Samuel 21:2"> And youi shall take the other ram; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; and his sons shall put their hands upon the head of the ram.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Isaiah 3:10; Ecclesiastes 8:12; Mishlei - Proverbs 11:18"> Then shall youi kill the ram, and take of his blood, and put &#91;it&#93; upon the tip of the right ear of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, and upon the tip of the right ear of his sons, and upon the thumb of their right hand, and upon the great toe of their right foot, and sprinkle the blood upon the altar round about.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="1 Kings 11:38; 1 Samuel 2:35; Tehillim - Psalms 37:3"> And youi shall take of the blood that &#91;is&#93; upon the altar, and of the anointing oil, and sprinkle &#91;it&#93; upon Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, and upon his garments, and upon his sons, and upon the garments of his sons with him: and he shall be consecrated, and his garments, and his sons, and his sons’ garments with him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:16; Exodus 1:16"> Also youi shall take of the ram the fat and the rump, and the fat that covers the inwards, and the caul (membrane; fatty tissue around a bodily organ) &#91;above&#93; the liver, and the two kidneys, and the fat that &#91;is&#93; upon them, and the right shoulder; for it &#91;is&#93; a ram of consecration:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And one loaf of bread, and one cake of oiled bread, and one wafer out of the basket of the unleavened bread that &#91;is&#93; before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate):</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And youi shall put all in the hands of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, and in the hands of his sons; and shall wave them &#91;for&#93; a wave offering before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And youi shall receive them of their hands, and burn &#91;them&#93; upon the altar for a burnt offering, for a soothing aroma before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): it &#91;is&#93; an offering made by fire unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> And youi shall take the breast of the ram of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;’s consecration, and wave it &#91;for&#93; a wave offering before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): and it shall be youi part.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> And youi shall sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) the breast of the wave offering, and the shoulder of the heave offering, which is waved, and which is heaved up, of the ram of the consecration, &#91;even&#93; of &#91;that&#93; which &#91;is&#93; for Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, and of &#91;that&#93; which is for his sons:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> And it shall be Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;’s and his sons’ by a statute forever from the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;: for it &#91;is&#93; an heave offering: and it shall be an heave offering from the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; of the sacrifice of their peace offerings, &#91;even&#93; their heave offering unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And the holy garments of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; shall be his sons’ after him, to be anointed in it, and to be consecrated in them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> &#91;And&#93; that son that is priest in his stead shall put them on seven days, when he comes into the tabernacle of the congregation to minister in the holy &#91;place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And youi shall take the ram of the consecration, and seethe his flesh in the holy place.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; and his sons shall eat the flesh of the ram, and the bread that &#91;is&#93; in the basket, &#91;by&#93; the door of the tabernacle of the congregation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And they shall eat those things which the atonement was made, to consecrate (treat as set apart or separate unto God) &#91;and&#93; to sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) them: but a stranger shall not eat &#91;thereof&#93;, because they &#91;are&#93; holy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> And if ought of the flesh of the consecrations, or of the bread, remain unto the morning, then youi shall burn the remainder with fire: it shall not be eaten, because it &#91;is&#93; holy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And thus shall youi do unto Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, and to his sons, according to all &#91;things&#93; which I have commanded youi: seven days shall youi consecrate (treat as set apart or separate unto God) them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And youi shall offer every day a bullock &#91;for&#93; a sin offering for atonement: and youi shall cleanse the altar, when youi have made an atonement for it, and youi shall anoint it, to sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> Seven days youi shall make an atonement for the altar, and sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) it; and it shall be an altar most holy: whatsoever touches the altar shall be holy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> Now this &#91;is that&#93; which youi shall offer upon the altar; two lambs of the first year day by day continually.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> The one lamb youi shall offer in the morning; and the other lamb youi shall offer at even:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> And with the one lamb a tenth deal of flour mingled with the fourth part of an hin &#91;a quart&#93; of beaten oil; and the fourth part of an hin &#91;a quart&#93; of wine &#91;for&#93; a drink offering.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> And the other lamb youi shall offer at even, and shall do thereto according to the meat offering of the morning, and according to the drink offering thereof, for a soothing aroma, an offering made by fire unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> &#91;This shall be&#93; a continual burnt offering throughout your generations &#91;at&#93; the door of the tabernacle of the congregation before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): where I will meet you, to speak there unto youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> And there I will meet with the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and &#91;the tabernacle&#93; shall be sanctified by my glory.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> And I will sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) the tabernacle of the congregation, and the altar: I will sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) also both Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; and his sons, to minister to me in the priest’s office.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45"> And I will dwell among the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and will be their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46"> And they shall know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, that brought them forth out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, that I may dwell among them: I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 2:1–2; Genesis 46:8–26; Genesis 35:23–26"> And youi shall make an altar to burn incense upon: &#91;of&#93; shittim wood shall youi make it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 35:22"> A cubit &#91;shall be&#93; the length thereof, and a cubit the breadth thereof; Foursquare (squared; four cornered) shall it be: and two cubits &#91;shall be&#93; the height thereof: the horns thereof &#91;shall be&#93; of the same.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 28:20; Genesis 35:23"> And youi shall overlay it with pure gold, the top thereof, and the sides thereof round about, and the horns thereof; and youi shall make unto it a crown of gold round about.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> And two golden rings shall youi make to it under the crown of it, by the two corners thereof, upon the two sides of it shall youi make &#91;it&#93;; and they shall be for places for the rods (clubs) to bear it withal.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Genesis 46:26–27; Judges 8:30"> And youi shall make the rods (clubs) &#91;of&#93; shittim wood, and overlay them with gold.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 50:26; Genesis 50:24; Acts 7:14–16"> And youi shall put it before the veil that &#91;is&#93; by the ark of the testimony, before the mercy seat that &#91;is&#93; over the testimony, where I will meet with youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Genesis 46:3; Genesis 1:28; Deuteronomy 26:5"> And Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; shall burn thereon sweet incense every morning: when he dresses the lamps, he shall burn incense upon it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Acts 7:18; Ecclesiastes 2:18–19; Ecclesiastes 9:15"> And when Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; lights the lamps at even, he shall burn incense upon it, a perpetual incense before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) throughout your generations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24–25; Ecclesiastes 4:4; Jacob 3:14–16"> You2f shall offer no strange incense thereon, nor burnt sacrifice, nor meat offering; neither shall you2f pour drink offering thereon.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Tehillim - Psalms 83:3–4; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:11"> And Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; shall make an atonement upon the horns of it once in a year with the blood of the sin offering of atonements: once in the year shall he make atonement upon it throughout your generations: it &#91;is&#93; most holy unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 15:13; Genesis 47:11; Exodus 3:7"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> When youi take the sum of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; after their number, then shall they give every man a ransom for his soul unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), when youi them; that there be no plague among them, when &#91;youi&#93; numbers them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:20"> This they shall give, every one that passes among them that are numbered, half a shekel after the shekel of the sanctuary (a shekel &#91;is&#93; twenty gerahs:) an half shekel &#91;shall be&#93; the offering of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Numbers 20:15; Exodus 2:23"> Khav’vah &#91;Eve&#93; (to breath, to live) every one that passes among them that are numbered, from twenty years old and above, shall give an offering unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> The rich shall not give more, and the poor shall not give less than half a shekel, when &#91;they&#93; give an offering unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), to make an atonement for your souls.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Exodus 1:22; Gosel 21:38; Revelation 12:4"> And youi shall take the atonement money of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, and shall appoint it for the service of the tabernacle of the congregation; that it may be a memorial unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), to make an atonement for your souls.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 5:29; Exodus 1:21; Gosel 10:28"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 8:4; 2 Samuel 13:28"> youi shall also make a Wash basin &#91;of&#93; brass, and his foot &#91;also of&#93; brass, to wash &#91;withal&#93;: and youi shall put it between the tabernacle of the congregation and the altar, and youi shall put water in it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 2:4–24; 2 Samuel 17:19–20; 1 Samuel 21:2"> For Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; and his sons shall wash their hands and their feet thereat:</span>

<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Isaiah 3:10; Ecclesiastes 8:12; Mishlei - Proverbs 11:18"> When they go into the tabernacle of the congregation, they shall wash with water, that they die not; or when they come near to the altar to minister, to burn offering made by fire unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate):</span>
</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Take youi also unto youi principal spices, of pure myrrh five hundred &#91;shekels&#93;, and of sweet cinnamon half so much, &#91;even&#93; two hundred and fifty &#91;shekels&#93;, and of sweet calamus two hundred and fifty &#91;shekels&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And of cassia five hundred &#91;shekels&#93;, after the shekel of the sanctuary, and of oil olive an hin &#91;a quart&#93;:</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> And youi shall anoint the tabernacle of the congregation with that, and the ark of the testimony,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> And the altar of burnt offering with all his vessels, and the Wash basin and his foot.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And youi shall sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) them, that they may be most holy: whatsoever touches them shall be holy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> And youi shall anoint Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; and his sons, and consecrate (treat as set apart or separate unto God) them, that &#91;they&#93; may minister unto me in the priest’s office.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And youi shall speak unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, saying, This shall be an holy anointing oil unto me throughout your generations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> Upon man’s flesh shall it not be poured, neither shall you2f make &#91;any other&#93; like it, after the composition of it: it &#91;is&#93; holy, &#91;and&#93; it shall be holy unto you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> Whosoever compounds &#91;any&#93; like it, or whosoever puts &#91;any&#93; of it upon a stranger, shall even be cut off from his people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, Take unto youi sweet spices, stacte, and onycha, and galbanum; &#91;these&#93; sweet spices with pure frankincense: of each shall there be a like &#91;weight&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And youi shall make it a perfume, a confection after the are of the apothecary, tempered together, pure &#91;and&#93; holy:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And youi shall beat &#91;some&#93; of it very small, and put of it before the testimony in the tabernacle of the congregation, where I will meet with youi: it shall be unto you most holy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> And &#91;as for&#93; the perfume which youi shall make, you2f shall not make to yourselves according to the composition thereof: it shall be unto youi holy for the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> Whosoever shall make like unto that, to smell thereto, shall even be cut off from his people.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:20"> Speak youi also unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, saying, Truly my Shabbat you2f shall keep: for it &#91;is&#93; a sign between me and you throughout your generations; that &#91;you2f&#93; may know that I &#91;am&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) that does sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) you.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Numbers 20:15; Exodus 2:23"> You2f shall keep the Shabbat &#91;Rest&#93; therefore; for it &#91;is&#93; holy unto you: every one that defiles it shall surely be put to death: for whosoever does &#91;any&#93; work in it, that soul shall be cut off from among his people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> Six days may work be done; but in the seventh &#91;is&#93; the Shabbat &#91;Rest&#93; of rest, holy to the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): whosoever does &#91;any&#93; work in the Shabbat &#91;Rest&#93; day, he shall surely be put to death.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Exodus 1:22; Gosel 21:38; Revelation 12:4"> For what reason the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; shall keep the Shabbat &#91;Rest&#93;, to observe the Shabbat &#91;Rest&#93; throughout their generations, &#91;for&#93; a perpetual covenant.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 5:29; Exodus 1:21; Gosel 10:28"> It &#91;is&#93; a sign between me and the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; forever: for &#91;in&#93; six days the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) made heaven and earth, and on the seventh day he rested, and was refreshed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 8:4; 2 Samuel 13:28"> And he gave unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, when he had made an end of communing with him upon mount Sinai, two tables of testimony, tables of stone, written with the finger of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 2:1–2; Genesis 46:8–26; Genesis 35:23–26"> And when the people saw that Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; delayed to come down out of the mount, the people gathered themselves together unto Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, and said unto him, Up, make us gods, which shall go before us; for &#91;as for&#93; this Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, the man that brought us up out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, we know not what is become of him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 35:22"> And Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; said unto them, Break off the golden earrings, which &#91;are&#93; in the ears of your wives, of your sons, and of your daughters, and bring &#91;them&#93; unto me.</span>
</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> And he received &#91;them&#93; at their hand, and fashioned it with a graving tool, after he had made it a molten calf: and they said, These &#91;be&#93; youi gods, O Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, which brought youi up out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 50:26; Genesis 50:24; Acts 7:14–16"> And they rose up early on the morrow, and offered burnt offerings, and brought peace offerings; and the people sat down to eat and to drink, and rose up to play.</span>
</div>

</div>

<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Acts 7:18; Ecclesiastes 2:18–19; Ecclesiastes 9:15"> They have turned aside quickly out of the way which I commanded them: they have made them a molten calf, and have worshipped it, and have sacrificed thereunto, and said, These &#91;be&#93; youi gods, O Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, which have brought youi up out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>

<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Tehillim - Psalms 83:3–4; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:11"> Now therefore let me alone, that my wrath may grow (become; advance) hot against them, and that I may consume them: and I will make of youi a great nation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 15:13; Genesis 47:11; Exodus 3:7"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; besought the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) his God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and said, LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), why does youi wrath grow (become; advance) hot against youi people, which youi have brought forth out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; with great power, and with a mighty hand?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> For what reason should the Mitzri speak, and say, For mischief did he bring them out, to slay them in the mountains, and to consume them from the face of the earth? Turn from youi fierce wrath, and repent of this evil against youi people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:20"> Remember Avraham &#91;Exalted Father&#93;, Yitz’chak &#91;Laughter&#93;, and Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, youri servants, to whom youi swore by youri own self, and said unto them, I will multiply your seed as the stars of heaven, and all this land that I have spoken of will I give unto your seed, and they shall inherit &#91;it&#93; forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Numbers 20:15; Exodus 2:23"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) repented of the evil which he thought to do unto his people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; turned, and went down from the mount, and the two tables of the testimony &#91;were&#93; in his hand: the tables &#91;were&#93; written on both their sides; on the one side and on the other &#91;were&#93; they written.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Exodus 1:22; Gosel 21:38; Revelation 12:4"> And the tables &#91;were&#93; the work of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and the writing &#91;was&#93; the writing of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, graven upon the tables.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 5:29; Exodus 1:21; Gosel 10:28"> And when Y’hoshua &#91;Yehovah is Salvation&#93; heard the noise of the people as they shouted, he said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, &#91;There is&#93; a noise of war in the camp.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 8:4; 2 Samuel 13:28"> And he said, &#91;It is&#93; not the voice of &#91;them that&#93; shout for mastery, neither &#91;is it&#93; the voice of &#91;them that&#93; cry for being overcome: &#91;but&#93; the noise of &#91;them that&#93; sing do I hear.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 2:4–24; 2 Samuel 17:19–20; 1 Samuel 21:2"> And it came to pass, as soon as he came near unto the camp, that he saw the calf, and the dancing: and Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;’ anger waxed hot, and he cast the tables out of his hands, and broke them beneath the mount.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Isaiah 3:10; Ecclesiastes 8:12; Mishlei - Proverbs 11:18"> And he took the calf which they had made, and burnt &#91;it&#93; in the fire, and ground &#91;it&#93; to powder, and scattered &#91;it&#93; upon the water, and made the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; drink &#91;of it&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="1 Kings 11:38; 1 Samuel 2:35; Tehillim - Psalms 37:3"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, What did this people unto youi, that youi have brought so great a sin upon them?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:16; Exodus 1:16"> And Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; said, Let not the anger of my lord grow (become; advance) hot: youi know the people, that they &#91;are set&#93; on mischief.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> For they said unto me, Make us gods, which shall go before us: for &#91;as for&#93; this Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, the man that brought us up out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, we know not what is become of him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And I said unto them, Whosoever has any gold, let them break &#91;it&#93; off. So they gave &#91;it&#93; me: then I cast it into the fire, and there came out this calf.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And when Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; saw that the people &#91;were&#93; naked; (for Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; had made them naked unto &#91;their&#93; shame among their enemies:)</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Then Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; stood in the gate of the camp, and said, Who &#91;is&#93; on the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; side? &#91;let him come&#93; unto me. And all the sons of Levi (Levite) &#91;Adhesion, Joiner&#93; gathered themselves together unto him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> And he said unto them, Thus says the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, Put every man his sword by his side, &#91;and&#93; go in and out from gate to gate throughout the camp, and slay every man his brother, and every man his companion, and every man his neighbour.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> And the children of Levi (Levite) &#91;Adhesion, Joiner&#93; did according to the word of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;: and there fell of the people that day about three thousand men.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> For Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; had said, Consecrate (treat as set apart or separate unto God) yourselves today to the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), even every man upon his son, and upon his brother; that he may bestow upon you a blessing this day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> And it came to pass on the morrow, that Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto the people, You2f have sinned a great sin: and now I will go up unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); perhaps I shall make an atonement for your sin.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; returned unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), and said, Oh, this people have sinned a great sin, and have made them gods of gold.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> Yet now, if youi will forgive their sin--; and if not, blot me, I pray youi, out of youi book which youi have written.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, Whosoever has sinned against me, him will I blot out of my book.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> Therefore now go, lead the people unto &#91;the place&#93; of which I have spoken unto youi: behold, my Angel shall go before youi: nevertheless in the day when I visit I will visit their sin upon them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) plagued the people, because they made the calf, which Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; made.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 2:1–2; Genesis 46:8–26; Genesis 35:23–26"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) said unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, Depart, &#91;and&#93; go up hence, youi and the people which youi have brought up out of the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, unto the land which I swear unto Avraham &#91;Exalted Father&#93;, to Yitz’chak &#91;Laughter&#93;, and to Ya’akov &#91;Heal of God&#93;, saying, Unto youi seed will I give it:</span>
