<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Isaiah 3:10; Ecclesiastes 8:12; Mishlei - Proverbs 11:18"> And all the congregation of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; departed from the presence of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="1 Kings 11:38; 1 Samuel 2:35; Tehillim - Psalms 37:3"> And they came, every one whose heart stirred him up, and every one whom his spirit made willing, &#91;and&#93; they brought the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; offering to the work of the tabernacle of the congregation, and for all his service, and for the holy garments.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:16; Exodus 1:16"> And they came, both men and women, as many as were willing hearted, &#91;and&#93; brought bracelets, and earrings, and rings, and tablets (small, flat ornaments or jewelry), all jewels of gold: and every man that offered &#91;offered&#93; an offering of gold unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And every man, with whom was found blue, and purple, and scarlet, and fine linen, and goats’ &#91;hair&#93;, and red skins of rams, and badgers’ skins, brought &#91;them&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> Every one that did offer an offering of silver and brass brought the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; offering: and every man, with whom was found shittim wood for any work of the service, brought &#91;it&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And all the women that were wise hearted did spin with their hands, and brought that which they had spun, &#91;both&#93; of blue, and of purple, &#91;and&#93; of scarlet, and of fine linen.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> And all the women whose heart stirred them up in wisdom spun goats’ &#91;hair&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> And the rulers brought onyx stones, and stones to be set, for the ephod (vest), and for the breastplate;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> And spice, and oil for the light, and for the anointing oil, and for the sweet incense.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> The people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; brought a willing offering unto the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), every man and woman, whose heart made them willing to bring for all manner of work, which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had commanded to be made by the hand of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; said unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, See, the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has called by name B’tzal’el &#91;In The Shadow (Protection) of El / God&#93; the son of Uri &#91;my light, my fire&#93;, the son of Hur &#91;Liberty&#93;, of the tribe of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And he has filled him with the spirit of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, in wisdom, in understanding, and in knowledge, and in all manner of workmanship;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And to devise detailed (skillfully made) works, to work in gold, and in silver, and in brass,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And in the cutting of stones, to set &#91;them&#93;, and in carving of wood, to make any manner of cunning work.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> And he has put in his heart that he may teach, &#91;both&#93; he, and Oholi’av &#91;Father’s Tent&#93;, the son of Achisamakh &#91;My Brother is Strength&#93;, of the tribe of Dan &#91;Judge&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> Them has he filled with wisdom of heart, to work all manner of work, of the engraver, and of the cunning workman, and of the embroiderer, in blue, and in purple, in scarlet, and in fine linen, and of the weaver, &#91;even&#93; of them that do any work, and of those that devise cunning work.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 2:1–2; Genesis 46:8–26; Genesis 35:23–26"> Then wrought B’tzal’el &#91;In The Shadow (Protection) of El / God&#93; and Oholi’av &#91;Father’s Tent&#93;, and every wise hearted man, in whom the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) put wisdom and understanding to know how to work all manner of work for the service of the sanctuary, according to all that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had commanded.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 35:22"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; called B’tzal’el &#91;In The Shadow (Protection) of El / God&#93; and Oholi’av &#91;Father’s Tent&#93;, and every wise hearted man, in whose heart the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had put wisdom, &#91;even&#93; every one whose heart stirred him up to come unto the work to do it:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 28:20; Genesis 35:23"> And they received of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; all the offering, which the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; had brought for the work of the service of the sanctuary, to make it &#91;withal&#93;. And they brought yet unto him free offerings every morning.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> And all the wise men, that wrought all the work of the sanctuary, came every man from his work which they made;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Genesis 46:26–27; Judges 8:30"> And they spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying, The people bring much more than enough for the service of the work, which the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded to make.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 50:26; Genesis 50:24; Acts 7:14–16"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; gave commandment, and they caused it to be proclaimed throughout the camp, saying, Let neither man nor woman make any more work for the offering of the sanctuary. So the people were restrained from bringing.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Genesis 46:3; Genesis 1:28; Deuteronomy 26:5"> For the stuff they had was sufficient for all the work to make it, and too much.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Acts 7:18; Ecclesiastes 2:18–19; Ecclesiastes 9:15"> And every wise hearted man among them that wrought the work of the tabernacle made ten curtains &#91;of&#93; fine twined linen, and blue, and purple, and scarlet: &#91;with&#93; Cherubim of cunning work made he them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24–25; Ecclesiastes 4:4; Jacob 3:14–16"> The length of one curtain &#91;was&#93; twenty and eight cubits, and the breadth of one curtain four cubits: the curtains &#91;were&#93; all of one size.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Tehillim - Psalms 83:3–4; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:11"> And he coupled the five curtains one unto another: and &#91;the other&#93; five curtains he coupled one unto another.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 15:13; Genesis 47:11; Exodus 3:7"> And he made loops of blue on the edge of one curtain from the selvedge (fabric edge to prevent unraveling) in the coupling: likewise he made in the uttermost side of &#91;another&#93; curtain, in the coupling of the second.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> Fifty loops made he in one curtain, and fifty loops made he in the edge of the curtain which &#91;was&#93; in the coupling of the second: the loops held one &#91;curtain&#93; to another.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:20"> And he made fifty hooks (fasteners) of gold, and coupled the curtains one unto another with the hooks (fasteners): so it became one tabernacle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Numbers 20:15; Exodus 2:23"> And he made curtains &#91;of&#93; goats’ &#91;hair&#93; for the tent over the tabernacle: eleven curtains he made them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> The length of one curtain &#91;was&#93; thirty cubits, and four cubits &#91;was&#93; the breadth of one curtain: the eleven curtains &#91;were&#93; of one size.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Exodus 1:22; Gosel 21:38; Revelation 12:4"> And he coupled five curtains by themselves, and six curtains by themselves.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 5:29; Exodus 1:21; Gosel 10:28"> And he made fifty loops upon the uttermost edge of the curtain in the coupling, and fifty loops made he upon the edge of the curtain which couples the second.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 8:4; 2 Samuel 13:28"> And he made fifty hooks (fasteners) &#91;of&#93; brass to couple the tent together, that it might be one.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 2:4–24; 2 Samuel 17:19–20; 1 Samuel 21:2"> And he made a covering for the tent &#91;of&#93; rams’ skins dyed red, and a covering &#91;of&#93; badgers’ skins above &#91;that&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Isaiah 3:10; Ecclesiastes 8:12; Mishlei - Proverbs 11:18"> And he made boards for the tabernacle &#91;of&#93; shittim wood, standing up.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="1 Kings 11:38; 1 Samuel 2:35; Tehillim - Psalms 37:3"> The length of a board &#91;was&#93; ten cubits, and the breadth of a board one cubit and a half.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:16; Exodus 1:16"> One board had two tenons, equally distant one from another: thus did he make for all the boards of the tabernacle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And he made boards for the tabernacle; twenty boards for the south side southward:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And forty sockets of silver he made under the twenty boards; two sockets under one board for his two tenons, and two sockets under another board for his two tenons.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And for the other side of the tabernacle, &#91;which is&#93; toward the north corner, he made twenty boards,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> And their forty sockets of silver; two sockets under one board, and two sockets under another board.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> And for the sides of the tabernacle westward he made six boards.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> And two boards made he for the corners of the tabernacle in the two sides.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And they were coupled beneath, and coupled together at the head thereof, to one ring: thus he did to both of them in both the corners.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> And there were eight boards; and their sockets &#91;were&#93; sixteen sockets of silver, under every board two sockets.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And he made bars of shittim wood; five for the boards of the one side of the tabernacle,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> And five bars for the boards of the other side of the tabernacle, and five bars for the boards of the tabernacle for the sides westward.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And he made the middle bar to shoot through the boards from the one end to the other.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> And he overlaid the boards with gold, and made their rings &#91;of&#93; gold &#91;to be&#93; places for the bars, and overlaid the bars with gold.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And he made a veil &#91;of&#93; blue, and purple, and scarlet, and fine twined linen: &#91;with&#93; Cherubim made he it of cunning work.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And he made thereunto four pillars &#91;of&#93; shittim &#91;wood&#93;, and overlaid them with gold: their hooks &#91;were of&#93; gold; and he cast for them four sockets of silver.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> And he made an hanging for the tabernacle door &#91;of&#93; blue, and purple, and scarlet, and fine twined linen, of needlework;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> And the five pillars of it with their hooks: and he overlaid their columns and their fillets with gold: but their five sockets &#91;were of&#93; brass.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 2:1–2; Genesis 46:8–26; Genesis 35:23–26"> And B’tzal’el &#91;In The Shadow (Protection) of El / God&#93; made the ark &#91;of&#93; shittim wood: two cubits and a half &#91;was&#93; the length of it, and a cubit and a half the breadth of it, and a cubit and a half the height of it:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 35:22"> And he overlaid it with pure gold within and without, and made a crown of gold to it round about.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 28:20; Genesis 35:23"> And he cast for it four rings of gold, &#91;to be set&#93; by the four corners of it; even two rings upon the one side of it, and two rings upon the other side of it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> And he made rods (clubs) &#91;of&#93; shittim wood, and overlaid them with gold.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Genesis 46:26–27; Judges 8:30"> And he put the rods (clubs) into the rings by the sides of the ark, to bear the ark.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 50:26; Genesis 50:24; Acts 7:14–16"> And he made the mercy seat &#91;of&#93; pure gold: two cubits and a half &#91;was&#93; the length thereof, and one cubit and a half the breadth thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Genesis 46:3; Genesis 1:28; Deuteronomy 26:5"> And he made two Cherubim &#91;of&#93; gold, beaten out of one piece made he them, on the two ends of the mercy seat;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Acts 7:18; Ecclesiastes 2:18–19; Ecclesiastes 9:15"> One cherub &#91;keruv&#93; on the end on this side, and another cherub &#91;keruv&#93; on the &#91;other&#93; end on that side: out of the mercy seat made he the Cherubim on the two ends thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24–25; Ecclesiastes 4:4; Jacob 3:14–16"> And the Cherubim spread out &#91;their&#93; wings on high, &#91;and&#93; covered with their wings over the mercy seat, with their faces one to another; &#91;even&#93; to the mercy seatward were the faces of the Cherubim.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Tehillim - Psalms 83:3–4; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:11"> And he made the table &#91;of&#93; shittim wood: two cubits &#91;was&#93; the length thereof, and a cubit the breadth thereof, and a cubit and a half the height thereof:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 15:13; Genesis 47:11; Exodus 3:7"> And he overlaid it with pure gold, and made thereunto a crown of gold round about.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> Also he made thereunto a border of an handbreadth round about; and made a crown of gold for the border thereof round about.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:20"> And he cast for it four rings of gold, and put the rings upon the four corners that &#91;were&#93; in the four feet thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Numbers 20:15; Exodus 2:23"> Over against the border were the rings, the places for the rods (clubs) to bear the table.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And he made the rods (clubs) &#91;of&#93; shittim wood, and overlaid them with gold, to bear the table.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Exodus 1:22; Gosel 21:38; Revelation 12:4"> And he made the vessels which &#91;were&#93; upon the table, his dishes, and his spoons, and his bowls, and his covers to cover withal, &#91;of&#93; pure gold.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 5:29; Exodus 1:21; Gosel 10:28"> And he made the candlestick &#91;of&#93; pure gold: &#91;of&#93; beaten work made he the candlestick; his shaft, and his branch, his bowls, his knops, and his flowers, were of the same:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 8:4; 2 Samuel 13:28"> And six branches going out of the sides thereof; three branches of the candlestick out of the one side thereof, and three branches of the candlestick out of the other side thereof:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 2:4–24; 2 Samuel 17:19–20; 1 Samuel 21:2"> Three bowls made after the fashion of almonds in one branch, a knob (flower bud) and a flower; and three bowls made like almonds in another branch, a knob (flower bud) and a flower: so throughout the six branches going out of the candlestick.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Isaiah 3:10; Ecclesiastes 8:12; Mishlei - Proverbs 11:18"> And in the candlestick &#91;were&#93; four bowls made like almonds, his knops, and his flowers:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="1 Kings 11:38; 1 Samuel 2:35; Tehillim - Psalms 37:3"> And a knob (flower bud) under two branches of the same, and a knob (flower bud) under two branches of the same, and a knob (flower bud) under two branches of the same, according to the six branches going out of it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:16; Exodus 1:16"> Their knops and their branches were of the same: all of it &#91;was&#93; one beaten work &#91;of&#93; pure gold.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And he made his seven lamps, and his snuffers, and his snuff dishes, &#91;of&#93; pure gold.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> &#91;Of&#93; a talent (weight of money; weight; gift) of pure gold made he it, and all the vessels thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And he made the incense altar &#91;of&#93; shittim wood: the length of it &#91;was&#93; a cubit, and the breadth of it a cubit; &#91;it was&#93; Foursquare (squared; four cornered); and two cubits &#91;was&#93; the height of it; the horns thereof were of the same.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> And he overlaid it with pure gold, &#91;both&#93; the top of it, and the sides thereof round about, and the horns of it: also he made unto it a crown of gold round about.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> And he made two rings of gold for it under the crown thereof, by the two corners of it, upon the two sides thereof, to be places for the rods (clubs) to bear it withal.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> And he made the rods (clubs) &#91;of&#93; shittim wood, and overlaid them with gold.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And he made the holy anointing oil, and the pure incense of sweet spices, according to the work of the apothecary.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 2:1–2; Genesis 46:8–26; Genesis 35:23–26"> And he made the altar of burnt offering &#91;of&#93; shittim wood: five cubits &#91;was&#93; the length thereof, and five cubits the breadth thereof; &#91;it was&#93; Foursquare (squared; four cornered); and three cubits the height thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 35:22"> And he made the horns thereof on the four corners of it; the horns thereof were of the same: and he overlaid it with brass.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 28:20; Genesis 35:23"> And he made all the vessels of the altar, the pots, and the shovels, and the basins, &#91;and&#93; the flesh hooks, and the firepans: all the vessels thereof made he &#91;of&#93; brass.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> And he made for the altar a brazen grate of network under the compass (go around, encircle; encompass) thereof beneath unto the midst of it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Genesis 46:26–27; Judges 8:30"> And he cast four rings for the four ends of the grate of brass, &#91;to be&#93; places for the rods (clubs).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 50:26; Genesis 50:24; Acts 7:14–16"> And he made the rods (clubs) &#91;of&#93; shittim wood, and overlaid them with brass.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Genesis 46:3; Genesis 1:28; Deuteronomy 26:5"> And he put the rods (clubs) into the rings on the sides of the altar, to bear it withal; he made the altar hollow with boards.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Acts 7:18; Ecclesiastes 2:18–19; Ecclesiastes 9:15"> And he made the Wash basin &#91;of&#93; brass, and the foot of it &#91;of&#93; brass, of the looking glasses of &#91;the women&#93; assembling, which assembled &#91;at&#93; the door of the tabernacle of the congregation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24–25; Ecclesiastes 4:4; Jacob 3:14–16"> And he made the court: on the south side southward the hangings of the court &#91;were of&#93; fine twined linen, an hundred cubits:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Tehillim - Psalms 83:3–4; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:11"> Their pillars &#91;were&#93; twenty, and their brazen sockets twenty; the hooks of the pillars and their fillets &#91;were of&#93; silver.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 15:13; Genesis 47:11; Exodus 3:7"> And for the north side &#91;the hangings were&#93; an hundred cubits, their pillars &#91;were&#93; twenty, and their sockets of brass twenty; the hooks of the pillars and their fillets &#91;of&#93; silver.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> And for the west side &#91;were&#93; hangings of fifty cubits, their pillars ten, and their sockets ten; the hooks of the pillars and their fillets &#91;of&#93; silver.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:20"> And for the east side eastward fifty cubits.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Numbers 20:15; Exodus 2:23"> The hangings of the one side &#91;of the gate were&#93; fifteen cubits; their pillars three, and their sockets three.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And for the other side of the court gate, on this hand and that hand, &#91;were&#93; hangings of fifteen cubits; their pillars three, and their sockets three.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Exodus 1:22; Gosel 21:38; Revelation 12:4"> All the hangings of the court round about &#91;were&#93; of fine twined linen.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 5:29; Exodus 1:21; Gosel 10:28"> And the sockets for the pillars &#91;were of&#93; brass; the hooks of the pillars and their fillets &#91;of&#93; silver; and the overlaying of their columns &#91;of&#93; silver; and all the pillars of the court &#91;were&#93; filleted with silver.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 8:4; 2 Samuel 13:28"> And the hanging for the gate of the court &#91;was&#93; needlework, &#91;of&#93; blue, and purple, and scarlet, and fine twined linen: and twenty cubits &#91;was&#93; the length, and the height in the breadth &#91;was&#93; five cubits, answerable to the hangings of the court.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 2:4–24; 2 Samuel 17:19–20; 1 Samuel 21:2"> And their pillars &#91;were&#93; four, and their sockets &#91;of&#93; brass four; their hooks &#91;of&#93; silver, and the overlaying of their columns and their fillets &#91;of&#93; silver.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Isaiah 3:10; Ecclesiastes 8:12; Mishlei - Proverbs 11:18"> And all the pins of the tabernacle, and of the court round about, &#91;were of&#93; brass.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="1 Kings 11:38; 1 Samuel 2:35; Tehillim - Psalms 37:3"> This is the sum of the tabernacle, &#91;even&#93; of the tabernacle of testimony, as it was counted, according to the commandment of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, &#91;for&#93; the service of the Levi’im (Levites) &#91;Joined to&#93;, by the hand of Itamar &#91;Coast of Palms&#93;, son to Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; the priest.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:16; Exodus 1:16"> And B’tzal’el &#91;In The Shadow (Protection) of El / God&#93; the son of Uri &#91;my light, my fire&#93;, the son of Hur &#91;Liberty&#93;, of the tribe of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, made all that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And with him &#91;was&#93; Oholi’av &#91;Father’s Tent&#93;, son of Achisamakh &#91;My Brother is Strength&#93;, of the tribe of Dan &#91;Judge&#93;, an engraver, and a cunning workman, and an embroiderer in blue, and in purple, and in scarlet, and fine linen.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> All the gold that was occupied for the work in all the work of the holy &#91;place&#93;, even the gold of the offering, was twenty and nine talents, and seven hundred and thirty shekels, after the shekel of the sanctuary.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And the silver of them that were numbered of the congregation &#91;was&#93; an hundred talents, and a thousand seven hundred and threescore and fifteen shekels, after the shekel of the sanctuary:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> A bekah for every man, &#91;that is&#93;, half a shekel, after the shekel of the sanctuary, for every one that went to be numbered, from twenty years old and upward, for six hundred thousand and three thousand and five hundred and fifty &#91;men&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> And of the hundred talents of silver were cast the sockets of the sanctuary, and the sockets of the veil; an hundred sockets of the hundred talents, a talent (weight of money; weight; gift) for a socket.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> And of the thousand seven hundred seventy and five shekels he made hooks for the pillars, and overlaid their columns, and filleted them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And the brass of the offering &#91;was&#93; seventy talents, and two thousand and four hundred shekels.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> And with that he made the sockets to the door of the tabernacle of the congregation, and the brazen altar, and the brazen grate for it, and all the vessels of the altar,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And the sockets of the court round about, and the sockets of the court gate, and all the pins of the tabernacle, and all the pins of the court round about.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 2:1–2; Genesis 46:8–26; Genesis 35:23–26"> And of the blue, and purple, and scarlet, they made cloths of service, to do service in the holy &#91;place&#93;, and made the holy garments for Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;; as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 35:22"> And he made the ephod (vest) &#91;of&#93; gold, blue, and purple, and scarlet, and fine twined linen.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 28:20; Genesis 35:23"> And they did beat the gold into thin plates, and cut &#91;it into&#93; wires, to work &#91;it&#93; in the blue, and in the purple, and in the scarlet, and in the fine linen, &#91;with&#93; cunning work.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> They made shoulder pieces for it, to couple &#91;it&#93; together: by the two edges was it coupled together.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Genesis 46:26–27; Judges 8:30"> And the detailed (skillfully made) belt of his ephod (vest), that &#91;was&#93; upon it, &#91;was&#93; of the same, according to the work thereof; &#91;of&#93; gold, blue, and purple, and scarlet, and fine twined linen; as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 50:26; Genesis 50:24; Acts 7:14–16"> And they wrought onyx stones enclosed in sockets of gold, graven, as signets are graven, with the names of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Genesis 46:3; Genesis 1:28; Deuteronomy 26:5"> And he put them on the shoulders of the ephod (vest), &#91;that they should be&#93; stones for a memorial to the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;; as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Acts 7:18; Ecclesiastes 2:18–19; Ecclesiastes 9:15"> And he made the breastplate &#91;of&#93; cunning work, like the work of the ephod (vest); &#91;of&#93; gold, blue, and purple, and scarlet, and fine twined linen.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24–25; Ecclesiastes 4:4; Jacob 3:14–16"> It was Foursquare (squared; four cornered); they made the breastplate double: a span &#91;was&#93; the length thereof, and a span the breadth thereof, &#91;being&#93; doubled.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Tehillim - Psalms 83:3–4; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:11"> And they set in it four rows of stones: &#91;the first&#93; row &#91;was&#93; a sardius, a topaz, and a Carbuncle (a red precious stone): this &#91;was&#93; the first row.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 15:13; Genesis 47:11; Exodus 3:7"> And the second row, an emerald, a sapphire, and a diamond.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> And the third row, a ligure, an agate, and an amethyst.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:20"> And the fourth row, a beryl, an onyx, and a jasper: &#91;they were&#93; enclosed in sockets of gold in their inclosings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Numbers 20:15; Exodus 2:23"> And the stones &#91;were&#93; according to the names of the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, twelve, according to their names, &#91;like&#93; the engravings of a signet (seal or stamp), everyone with his name, according to the twelve tribes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And they made upon the breastplate chains at the ends, &#91;of&#93; wreathen work &#91;of&#93; pure gold.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Exodus 1:22; Gosel 21:38; Revelation 12:4"> And they made two sockets &#91;of&#93; gold, and two gold rings; and put the two rings in the two ends of the breastplate.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 5:29; Exodus 1:21; Gosel 10:28"> And they put the two wreathen chains of gold in the two rings on the ends of the breastplate.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 8:4; 2 Samuel 13:28"> And the two ends of the two wreathen chains they fastened in the two sockets, and put them on the shoulder pieces of the ephod (vest), before it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 2:4–24; 2 Samuel 17:19–20; 1 Samuel 21:2"> And they made two rings of gold, and put &#91;them&#93; on the two ends of the breastplate, upon the border of it, which &#91;was&#93; on the side of the ephod (vest) inward.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Isaiah 3:10; Ecclesiastes 8:12; Mishlei - Proverbs 11:18"> And they made two &#91;other&#93; golden rings, and put them on the two sides of the ephod (vest) underneath, toward the forepart of it, over against the &#91;other&#93; coupling thereof, above the detailed (skillfully made) belt of the ephod (vest).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="1 Kings 11:38; 1 Samuel 2:35; Tehillim - Psalms 37:3"> And they did bind the breastplate by his rings unto the rings of the ephod (vest) with a lace of blue, that it might be above the detailed (skillfully made) belt of the ephod (vest), and that the breastplate might not be loosed from the ephod (vest); as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:16; Exodus 1:16"> And he made the robe of the ephod (vest) &#91;of&#93; woven work, all &#91;of&#93; blue.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And &#91;there was&#93; an hole in the midst of the robe, as the hole of an habergeon (coat of mail for the neck and down), &#91;with&#93; a band round about the hole, that it should not rend (tear apart; forcefully rip apart).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And they made upon the hems of the robe pomegranates &#91;of&#93; blue, and purple, and scarlet, &#91;and&#93; twined &#91;linen&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And they made bells &#91;of&#93; pure gold, and put the bells between the pomegranates upon the hem of the robe, round about between the pomegranates;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> A bell and a pomegranate, a bell and a pomegranate, round about the hem of the robe to minister &#91;in&#93;; as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> And they made coats &#91;of&#93; fine linen &#91;of&#93; woven work for Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;, and for his sons,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> And a mitre (turban; ceremonial headdress) &#91;of&#93; fine linen, and goodly bonnets &#91;of&#93; fine linen, and linen trousers &#91;of&#93; fine twined linen,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And a belt &#91;of&#93; fine twined linen, and blue, and purple, and scarlet, &#91;of&#93; needlework; as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> And they made the plate of the holy crown &#91;of&#93; pure gold, and wrote upon it a writing, &#91;like to&#93; the engravings of a signet (seal or stamp), HOLINESS TO THE LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And they tied unto it a lace of blue, to fasten &#91;it&#93; on high upon the mitre (turban; ceremonial headdress); as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> Thus was all the work of the tabernacle of the tent of the congregation finished: and the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; did according to all that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, so did they.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And they brought the tabernacle unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, the tent, and all his furniture, his hooks (fasteners), his boards, his bars, and his pillars, and his sockets,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> And the covering of rams’ skins dyed red, and the covering of badgers’ skins, and the veil of the covering,</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> The ark of the testimony, and the rods (clubs) thereof, and the mercy seat,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> The table, &#91;and&#93; all the vessels thereof, and the showbread,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> The pure candlestick, &#91;with&#93; the lamps thereof, &#91;even with&#93; the lamps to be set in order, and all the vessels thereof, and the oil for light,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> And the golden altar, and the anointing oil, and the sweet incense, and the hanging for the tabernacle door,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> The brazen altar, and his grate of brass, his rods (clubs), and all his vessels, the Wash basin and his foot,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> The hangings of the court, his pillars, and his sockets, and the hanging for the court gate, his cords, and his pins, and all the vessels of the service of the tabernacle, for the tent of the congregation,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> The cloths of service to do service in the holy &#91;place&#93;, and the holy garments for Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; the priest, and his sons’ garments, to minister in the priest’s office.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> According to all that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, so the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; made all the work.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; did look upon all the work, and, behold, they had done it as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had commanded, even so had they done it: and Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="1 Chronicles 2:1–2; Genesis 46:8–26; Genesis 35:23–26"> And the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) spoke unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, saying,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Genesis 35:22"> On the first day of the first month shall youi set up the tabernacle of the tent of the congregation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Exodus 28:20; Genesis 35:23"> And youi shall put in it the ark of the testimony, and cover the ark with the veil.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4"> And youi shall bring in the table, and set in order the things that are to be set in order upon it; and youi shall bring in the candlestick, and light the lamps thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Deuteronomy 10:22; Genesis 46:26–27; Judges 8:30"> And youi shall set the altar of gold for the incense before the ark of the testimony, and put the hanging of the door to the tabernacle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="Genesis 50:26; Genesis 50:24; Acts 7:14–16"> And youi shall set the altar of the burnt offering before the door of the tabernacle of the tent of the congregation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7" data-xref="Genesis 46:3; Genesis 1:28; Deuteronomy 26:5"> And youi shall set the Wash basin between the tent of the congregation and the altar, and shall put water in it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8" data-xref="Acts 7:18; Ecclesiastes 2:18–19; Ecclesiastes 9:15"> And youi shall set up the court round about, and hang up the hanging at the court gate.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24–25; Ecclesiastes 4:4; Jacob 3:14–16"> And youi shall take the anointing oil, and anoint the tabernacle, and all that &#91;is&#93; in it, and shall hallow (to approve, commend, accept) it, and all the vessels thereof: and it shall be holy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Tehillim - Psalms 83:3–4; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:11"> And youi shall anoint the altar of the burnt offering, and all his vessels, and sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) the altar: and it shall be an altar most holy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11" data-xref="Genesis 15:13; Genesis 47:11; Exodus 3:7"> And youi shall anoint the Wash basin and his foot, and sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 105:24; Exodus 1:9; Romans 8:28"> And youi shall bring Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; and his sons unto the door of the tabernacle of the congregation, and wash them with water.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13" data-xref="Deuteronomy 4:20"> And youi shall put upon Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; the holy garments, and anoint him, and sanctify (make holy; set apart for God’s use) him; that he may minister unto me in the priest’s office.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Numbers 20:15; Exodus 2:23"> And youi shall bring his sons, and clothe them with coats:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And youi shall anoint them, as youi did anoint their father, that they may minister unto me in the priest’s office: for their anointing shall surely be an everlasting priesthood throughout their generations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16" data-xref="Exodus 1:22; Gosel 21:38; Revelation 12:4"> Thus did Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;: according to all that the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded him, so did he.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17" data-xref="Acts 5:29; Exodus 1:21; Gosel 10:28"> And it came to pass in the first month in the second year, on the first &#91;day&#93; of the month, &#91;that&#93; the tabernacle was reared up.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18" data-xref="Ecclesiastes 8:4; 2 Samuel 13:28"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; reared up the tabernacle, and fastened his sockets, and set up the boards thereof, and put in the bars thereof, and reared up his pillars.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19" data-xref="Joshua 2:4–24; 2 Samuel 17:19–20; 1 Samuel 21:2"> And he spread abroad the tent over the tabernacle, and put the covering of the tent above upon it; as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20" data-xref="Isaiah 3:10; Ecclesiastes 8:12; Mishlei - Proverbs 11:18"> And he took and put the testimony into the ark, and set the rods (clubs) on the ark, and put the mercy seat above upon the ark:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21" data-xref="1 Kings 11:38; 1 Samuel 2:35; Tehillim - Psalms 37:3"> And he brought the ark into the tabernacle, and set up the veil of the covering, and covered the ark of the testimony; as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22" data-xref="Acts 7:19; Mishlei - Proverbs 1:16; Exodus 1:16"> And he put the table in the tent of the congregation, upon the side of the tabernacle northward, without the veil.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And he set the bread in order upon it before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) had commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And he put the candlestick in the tent of the congregation, over against the table, on the side of the tabernacle southward.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> And he lighted the lamps before the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate); as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> And he put the golden altar in the tent of the congregation before the veil:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> And he burnt sweet incense thereon; as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> And he set up the hanging &#91;at&#93; the door of the tabernacle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> And he put the altar of burnt offering &#91;by&#93; the door of the tabernacle of the tent of the congregation, and offered upon it the burnt offering and the meat offering; as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> And he set the Wash basin between the tent of the congregation and the altar, and put water there, to wash &#91;withal&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; and his sons washed their hands and their feet thereat:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> When they went into the tent of the congregation, and when they came near unto the altar, they washed; as the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) commanded Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> And he reared up the court round about the tabernacle and the altar, and set up the hanging of the court gate. So Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; finished the work.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> Then a cloud covered the tent of the congregation, and the glory of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) filled the tabernacle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; was not able to enter into the tent of the congregation, because the cloud abode thereon, and the glory of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) filled the tabernacle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And when the cloud was taken up from over the tabernacle, the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93; went onward in all their journeys:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> But if the cloud were not taken up, then they journeyed not till the day that it was taken up.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> For the cloud of the LORD-Yehōvah (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) &#91;was&#93; upon the tabernacle by day, and fire was on it by night, in the sight of all the house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel Of God&#93;, throughout all their journeys.</span>
</div>
</div>

</div>
